} }, update(dt){ if(this.timer>0){ this.timer-=dt; } else{ this.count=0; } } }; //================================================ // ADVANCED ATTACK //================================================ function playerAttack(){ let damage= Player.totalAttack || Player.attack; enemy.x+= (dx/distance)*50; enemy.y+= (dy/distance)*50; createDamageText( "-"+Math.floor(damage), enemy.x, enemy.y ); createEffect( enemy.x, enemy.y, "hit" ); Combo.hit(); if(enemy.hp<=0) killEnemy(enemy); } } } //================================================ // SKILL BAR HUD //================================================ const HUD={ draw(){ ctx.fillStyle= "rgba(0,0,0,.5)"; ctx.fillRect( 20, canvas.height-100, 500, 70 ); ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.font="20px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Q Potion", 40, canvas.height-60 ); ctx.fillText( "1 Slash", 180, canvas.height-60 ); ctx.fillText( "2 Fire", 300, canvas.height-60 ); ctx.fillText( "3 Heal", 420, canvas.height-60 ); } }; //================================================ // PLAYER STATUS HUD //================================================ function drawPlayerStatus(){ ctx.fillStyle="black"; ctx.fillRect( 20, 20, 250, 90 ); ctx.fillText( "LV "+Player.level, 40, 100 ); } //================================================ // KEY COMBAT //================================================ window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if(GameState.screen!="world") return; //================================================ // LOAD PLAYER SPRITE //================================================ function loadPlayerSprite(src){ PlayerSprite.image= new Image(); PlayerSprite.image.src=src; } //================================================ // ANIMATION DATABASE //================================================ const AnimationData={ idle:{ frames:[0,1,2,3], speed:0.2 }, walk:{ frames:[4,5,6,7], speed:0.12 }, attack:{ frames:[8,9,10], speed:0.08 }, skill:{ frames:[11,12,13,14], speed:0.1 } }; //================================================ // UPDATE ANIMATION //================================================ function updatePlayerAnimation(dt){ let anim= AnimationData[ PlayerSprite.animation ]; } } } //================================================ // DRAW PLAYER PIXEL //================================================ function drawPlayer(){ if(!PlayerSprite.image) return; let anim= AnimationData[ PlayerSprite.animation ]; } //================================================ // PLAYER MOVEMENT ANIMATION //================================================ function updatePlayerAnimationState(){ if(Player.attacking){ PlayerSprite.animation="attack"; return; } if( Player.moving ){ PlayerSprite.animation="walk"; } else{ PlayerSprite.animation="idle"; } } //================================================ // PLAYER MOVEMENT SYSTEM //================================================ const Input={ up:false, down:false, left:false, right:false }; if(e.key=="s") Input.down=false; if(e.key=="a") Input.left=false; if(e.key=="d") Input.right=false; }); function movePlayer(dt){ let speed= Player.speed || 250; Player.moving=false; if(Input.up){ Player.y-=speed*dt; Player.moving=true; } if(Input.down){ Player.y+=speed*dt; Player.moving=true; } if(Input.left){ Player.x-=speed*dt; Player.moving=true; } if(Input.right){ Player.x+=speed*dt; Player.moving=true; } updatePlayerAnimationState(); } //================================================ // CAMERA SYSTEM //================================================ const Camera={ x:0, y:0, smooth:0.1 }; function updateCamera(){ Camera.x += ( Player.x- canvas.width/2 - Camera.x ) * Camera.smooth; class CharacterSprite{ constructor(name){ this.name=name; this.frame=0; this.frameTime=0; this.animation="idle"; this.speed=8; } update(dt){ this.frameTime+=dt; if(this.frameTime> 1/this.speed){ this.frame++; } //================================================ // PARTICLE EFFECT ENGINE //================================================ const Particles=[]; class Particle{ constructor(x,y,type){ this.x=x; this.y=y; this.type=type; this.life=50; this.size=10; this.speed= Math.random()*5; } update(){ this.y-=this.speed; this.life--; } draw(){ ctx.font="20px Arial"; if(this.type=="fire") ctx.fillText( "🔥", this.x-Camera.x, this.y-Camera.y ); if(this.type=="magic") ctx.fillText( "✨", this.x-Camera.x, this.y-Camera.y ); } } function createParticle(x,y,type){ Particles.push( new Particle( x, y, type ) ); } function updateParticles(){ for(let p of Particles){ p.update(); } for(let i= Particles.length-1; i>=0; i--){ if(Particles[i].life<=0) Particles.splice(i,1); } } function drawParticles(){ for(let p of Particles){ p.draw(); } } //================================================ // SMOOTH CAMERA SYSTEM //================================================ const Camera={ x:0, y:0, speed:5 }; function updateCamera(){ let targetX= Player.x- canvas.width/2; let targetY= Player.y- canvas.height/2; Camera.x+= (targetX-Camera.x) * 0.1; Camera.y+= (targetY-Camera.y) * 0.1; } //================================================ // COLLISION PHYSICS //================================================ const Collision={ objects:[] }; function checkCollision(a,b){ let dx= a.x-b.x; let dy= a.y-b.y; let distance= Math.sqrt( dx*dx+ dy*dy ); return distance<40; } function addCollisionObject(obj){ Collision.objects.push(obj); } function updateCollision(){ for(let obj of Collision.objects){ if( checkCollision( Player, obj ) ){ Player.x-=10; Player.y-=10; } } } //================================================ // NPC MOVEMENT AI //================================================ class NPCWalker{ constructor(npc){ this.npc=npc; this.targetX=npc.x; this.targetY=npc.y; } update(){ let dx= this.targetX- this.npc.x; let dy= this.targetY- this.npc.y; let d= Math.sqrt( dx*dx+ dy*dy ); if(d<5){ this.targetX= this.npc.x+ (Math.random()*400-200); this.targetY= this.npc.y+ (Math.random()*400-200); } else{ this.npc.x+= dx/d*1; this.npc.y+= dy/d*1; } } } //================================================ // ENEMY PATH FINDING //================================================ function enemyPathFind(enemy){ let dx= Player.x-enemy.x; let dy= Player.y-enemy.y; let d= Math.sqrt( dx*dx+ dy*dy ); if(d>0){ enemy.x+= dx/d*2; enemy.y+= dy/d*2; } } //================================================ // DROP ITEM VISUAL //================================================ const GroundItems=[]; class GroundItem{ constructor(item,x,y){ this.item=item; this.x=x; this.y=y; this.bounce=0; } update(){ this.bounce+=0.05; } draw(){ ctx.fillStyle="gold"; ctx.fillRect( this.x-Camera.x, this.y-Camera.y, 20, 20 ); } } function dropItem(item,x,y){ GroundItems.push( new GroundItem( item, x, y ) ); } function collectGroundItem(){ for(let i= GroundItems.length-1; i>=0; i--){ let item= GroundItems[i]; if( checkCollision( Player, item ) ){ Inventory.add( { name:item.item, amount:1 } ); } //================================================ // TUTORIAL SYSTEM //================================================ const Tutorial={ step:0, finished:false }; const TutorialText=[ "Use WASD to Move", "Press Mouse to Attack", "Find NPC for Quest", "Collect Equipment", "Upgrade Your Hero" ]; function equipWeapon(name){ let weapon= WeaponDatabase[name]; if(!weapon) return; Equipment.weapon={ name:name, damage:weapon.damage, rarity:weapon.rarity }; Player.attack+=weapon.damage; } //================================================ // PLAYER ATTACK SYSTEM //================================================ const AttackSystem={ cooldown:0, range:80, damage:100 }; let distance= Math.sqrt( dx*dx+ dy*dy ); if(distance{ HitBox.active=false; },100); } //================================================ // SKILL EFFECT SYSTEM //================================================ function skillEffect(type,x,y){ if(type=="fire"){ for(let i=0;i<20;i++){ createParticle( x+ Math.random()*100-50, y+ Math.random()*100-50, "fire" ); } } if(type=="wind"){ for(let i=0;i<20;i++){ createParticle( x, y, "magic" ); } } } //================================================ // INVENTORY DRAG DROP //================================================ const DragItem={ active:false, item:null }; window.addEventListener( "keyup", e=>{ Input.keys[e.key.toLowerCase()]=false; }); canvas.addEventListener( "mousemove", e=>{ let rect= canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); Input.mouse.x= e.clientX-rect.left; } }); //================================================ // PLAYER MOVEMENT //================================================ Player.speed=300; function updatePlayerMovement(dt){ let moveX=0; let moveY=0; //================================================ // CHARACTER CLASS SYSTEM //================================================ const ClassDatabase={ Warrior:{ hp:3000, mana:500, attack:300, defense:200, skills:[ "PowerSlash", "ShieldWall", "EarthSmash" ] }, Mage:{ hp:1500, mana:3000, attack:500, defense:80, skills:[ "FireBall", "IceStorm", "Meteor" ] }, Archer:{ hp:2000, mana:1000, attack:400, defense:120, skills:[ "ArrowRain", "MultiShot", "PoisonArrow" ] } }; function rollRarity(){ let r= Math.random()*100; let total=0; for(let type in Rarity){ total+= Rarity[type].rate; if(r<=total) return type; } return "Common"; } //================================================ // ITEM DATABASE //================================================ const ItemDatabase={ Sword:{ damage:100 }, Armor:{ defense:100 }, Gem:{ value:500 }, MagicCrystal:{ power:200 } }; //================================================ // RANDOM ITEM DROP //================================================ function randomDrop(enemy){ let chance= Math.random()*100; if(chance>70) return; let names=[ "Sword", "Armor", "Gem", "MagicCrystal" ]; let item= names[ Math.floor( Math.random()*names.length ) ]; let rarity= rollRarity(); let drop={ name: rarity+" "+item, rarity:rarity, amount:1 }; function spawnZoneMonster(zone){ let amount= zone.level/5+5; for(let i=0;i{ initializeGame(); }; //================================================ // PLAYER STATUS SYSTEM //================================================ Player.level=1; Player.exp=0; Player.expNeed=1000; Player.maxLevel=100; Player.hp=3000; Player.maxHp=3000; Player.mana=1000; Player.maxMana=1000; Player.dead=false; Player.respawnPoint={ x:500, y:500 }; //================================================ // EXPERIENCE SYSTEM //================================================ function addExp(amount){ if(Player.level>=Player.maxLevel) return; Player.exp+=amount; addFloatText( "+"+amount+" EXP", Player.x, Player.y, "cyan" ); checkLevelUp(); } function checkLevelUp(){ while( Player.exp>=Player.expNeed ){ Player.exp-=Player.expNeed; levelUp(); } } function levelUp(){ Player.level++; Player.expNeed= Math.floor( Player.expNeed*1.25 ); Player.maxHp+=500; Player.maxMana+=200; Player.attack+=100; Player.defense+=50; Player.hp= Player.maxHp; Player.mana= Player.maxMana; createParticle( Player.x, Player.y, "magic" ); addFloatText( "LEVEL UP", Player.x, Player.y, "gold" ); } //================================================ // PLAYER DAMAGE SYSTEM //================================================ function playerTakeDamage(amount){ if(Player.dead) return; GameState.screen= GameScreen.PLAYING; } //================================================ // MAIN UPDATE ENGINE //================================================ function engineUpdate(dt){ if(GameState.screen!= GameScreen.PLAYING) return; //========================= // COMPANION //========================= class Companion{ constructor(name,job){ this.name=name; this.job=job; this.level=1; this.hp=150; this.maxHp=150; this.attack=18; this.defense=6; if(!item)continue; Player.attack+= item.attack; Player.defense+= item.defense; Player.maxHp+= item.hp; Player.moveSpeed+= item.speed; Player.critRate= (Player.critRate||0)+ item.crit; } } //========================= // SET BONUS //========================= function updateCostumeSet(){ let count=0; for( const key in CostumeSystem.equipped ){ if( CostumeSystem .equipped[key] ){ count++; } } if(count>=4){ Player.attack+=40; Player.maxHp+=300; } if(count>=8){ Player.attack+=120; Player.maxHp+=800; Player.critRate+=8; } } //========================= // COLLECTION //========================= function updateCostumeCollection(){ CostumeSystem.collectionBonus= CostumeSystem.inventory.length; Player.attack+= CostumeSystem .collectionBonus; } //========================= // DRAW PLAYER //========================= function drawCostume(){ const body= CostumeSystem.equipped.body; if(body){ ctx.fillStyle="#3399ff"; ctx.fillRect( Player.x-10, Player.y-18, 20, 22 ); } const head= CostumeSystem.equipped.head; if(head){ ctx.fillStyle="#ffaa00"; ctx.fillRect( Player.x-9, Player.y-32, 18, 10 ); } const aura= CostumeSystem.equipped.aura; if(aura){ ctx.strokeStyle="#00ffff"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc( Player.x, Player.y, 28, 0, Math.PI*2 ); ctx.stroke(); } } //========================= // WINDOW //========================= window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if( e.key.toLowerCase()=="h" ){ CostumeSystem.open= !CostumeSystem.open; } }); function drawCostumeUI(){ if( !CostumeSystem.open )return; ctx.fillStyle= "rgba(0,0,0,.92)"; ctx.fillRect( 120, 40, 760, 620 ); ctx.strokeStyle="#FF66CC"; ctx.strokeRect( 120, 40, 760, 620 ); ctx.fillStyle="gold"; ctx.font="28px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Costume", 160, 80 ); let y=130; ctx.font="18px Arial"; for( const c of CostumeSystem.inventory ){ ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.fillText( c.name, 160, y ); ctx.fillText( c.slot, 400, y ); ctx.fillText( c.rarity, 560, y ); y+=28; } } //========================= // DEFAULT //========================= const body=createCostume( "Traveler Outfit", "body", "Rare" ); body.hp=120; body.defense=15; const hat=createCostume( "Golden Crown", "head", "Epic" ); hat.attack=15; hat.crit=2; const aura=createCostume( "Blue Aura", "aura", "Legendary" ); aura.attack=20; aura.hp=150; equipCostume( body.id ); equipCostume( hat.id ); equipCostume( aura.id ); //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateCostume(){ updateCostumeBonus(); updateCostumeSet(); updateCostumeCollection(); } //========================= // ELEMENT SYSTEM //========================= const ElementSystem={ types:[ "Neutral", "Fire", "Water", "Wind", "Earth", "Light", "Dark", "Ice", "Thunder", "Poison" ], advantage:{ Fire:"Wind", Wind:"Earth", Earth:"Water", Water:"Fire", Light:"Dark", Dark:"Light", Ice:"Fire", Thunder:"Water", Poison:"Earth" } }; //========================= // PLAYER ELEMENT //========================= Player.element="Neutral"; Player.elementAttack=0; Player.elementResist={ Fire:0, Water:0, Wind:0, Earth:0, Light:0, Dark:0, Ice:0, Thunder:0, Poison:0 }; //========================= // ENEMY ELEMENT //========================= function assignEnemyElement(enemy){ enemy.element= ElementSystem.types[ Math.floor( Math.random()* ElementSystem.types.length ) ]; } //========================= // DAMAGE //========================= function calculateElementDamage( damage, attacker, target ){ let result=damage; if( ElementSystem .advantage[ attacker.element ]== target.element ){ result*=1.30; } if( ElementSystem .advantage[ target.element ]== attacker.element ){ result*=0.70; } if( target.elementResist&& target.elementResist[ attacker.element ] ){ result*= 1- target .elementResist[ attacker.element ]/100; } return Math.floor(result); } //========================= // ENCHANT //========================= function enchantElement( type ){ Player.element=type; } //========================= // DRAW ICON //========================= function drawElementIcon(){ const color={ Neutral:"#cccccc", Fire:"#ff5533", Water:"#33aaff", Wind:"#66ff66", Earth:"#aa7733", Light:"#ffff88", Dark:"#8844ff", Ice:"#99ffff", Thunder:"#ffff00", Poison:"#44ff44" }; ctx.fillStyle= color[ Player.element ]; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc( 70, 80, 12, 0, Math.PI*2 ); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.font="16px Arial"; ctx.fillText( Player.element, 90, 86 ); } //========================= // DROP STONE //========================= function elementStoneDrop(){ if( Math.random()>0.015 )return; const type= ElementSystem.types[ 1+ Math.floor( Math.random()*9 ) ]; Inventory.add({ id:Date.now(), type:"ElementStone", name: type+ " Stone", rarity:"Epic", element:type, sell:5000 }); } //========================= // APPLY STONE //========================= function useElementStone( item ){ if( item.type!= "ElementStone" )return; enchantElement( item.element ); } //========================= // RESIST //========================= function addElementResist( type, value ){ if( Player .elementResist[type] ==null )return; Player .elementResist[type]+= value; } //========================= // MONSTER BONUS //========================= function monsterElementReward( enemy ){ if( enemy.element== "Fire" ){ Player.attack+=1; } if( enemy.element== "Water" ){ Player.maxHp+=3; } } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateElement(){ drawElementIcon(); } //========================= // AUTO BATTLE SYSTEM //========================= const AutoBattle={ enable:false, autoSkill:true, autoPotion:true, autoLoot:true, searchRange:450, target:null }; //========================= // TOGGLE //========================= window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if( e.key=="F8" ){ AutoBattle.enable= !AutoBattle.enable; } }); //========================= // FIND TARGET //========================= function autoFindTarget(){ let best=null; let bestDist=999999; for( const enemy of Enemies ){ if( enemy.dead )continue; const d=Math.hypot( enemy.x- Player.x, enemy.y- Player.y ); if( d< AutoBattle.searchRange&& d80 ){ Player.x+= dx/dist* Player.moveSpeed* dt; Player.y+= dy/dist* Player.moveSpeed* dt; } } //========================= // ATTACK //========================= function autoAttack(){ if( !AutoBattle.target )return; if( Attack.cooldown<=0 ){ Attack.active=true; Attack.x= Player.x; Attack.y= Player.y; } } //========================= // SKILL //========================= function autoSkill(){ if( !AutoBattle.autoSkill )return; for( const skill of SkillBar ){ if( !skill )continue; if( skill.cooldown<=0 ){ castSkill( skill.id ); break; } } } //========================= // POTION //========================= function autoPotion(){ if( !AutoBattle.autoPotion )return; if( Player.hp< Player.maxHp*0.4 ){ usePotion( "hp" ); } if( Player.mp< Player.maxMp*0.3 ){ usePotion( "mp" ); } } //========================= // AUTO LOOT //========================= function autoLoot(){ if( !AutoBattle.autoLoot )return; for( const drop of Drops ){ if( Math.hypot( drop.x- Player.x, drop.y- Player.y )<100 ){ pickupDrop( drop ); } } } //========================= // MAIN //========================= function updateAutoBattle(dt){ if( !AutoBattle.enable )return; autoFindTarget(); autoMove(dt); autoAttack(); autoSkill(); autoPotion(); autoLoot(); } //========================= // UI //========================= function drawAutoBattle(){ ctx.fillStyle= AutoBattle.enable? "#00ff66": "#ff3333"; ctx.font="18px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Auto : "+ ( AutoBattle.enable? "ON": "OFF" ), 20, 30 ); if( AutoBattle.target ){ ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.fillText( "Target : "+ AutoBattle.target.name, 20, 55 ); } } //========================= // AUTO FARM //========================= function autoFarm(){ if( !AutoBattle.enable )return; if( AutoBattle.target )return; const angle= Math.random()* Math.PI*2; Player.x+= Math.cos(angle)*80; Player.y+= Math.sin(angle)*80; } //========================= // AUTO BATTLE SYSTEM //========================= const AutoBattle={ enable:false, autoSkill:true, autoPotion:true, autoLoot:true, searchRange:450, target:null }; //========================= // TOGGLE //========================= window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if( e.key=="F8" ){ AutoBattle.enable= !AutoBattle.enable; } }); //========================= // FIND TARGET //========================= function autoFindTarget(){ let best=null; let bestDist=999999; for( const enemy of Enemies ){ if( enemy.dead )continue; const d=Math.hypot( enemy.x- Player.x, enemy.y- Player.y ); if( d< AutoBattle.searchRange&& d80 ){ Player.x+= dx/dist* Player.moveSpeed* dt; Player.y+= dy/dist* Player.moveSpeed* dt; } } //========================= // ATTACK //========================= function autoAttack(){ if( !AutoBattle.target )return; if( Attack.cooldown<=0 ){ Attack.active=true; Attack.x= Player.x; Attack.y= Player.y; } } //========================= // SKILL //========================= function autoSkill(){ if( !AutoBattle.autoSkill )return; for( const skill of SkillBar ){ if( !skill )continue; if( skill.cooldown<=0 ){ castSkill( skill.id ); break; } } } //========================= // POTION //========================= function autoPotion(){ if( !AutoBattle.autoPotion )return; if( Player.hp< Player.maxHp*0.4 ){ usePotion( "hp" ); } if( Player.mp< Player.maxMp*0.3 ){ usePotion( "mp" ); } } //========================= // AUTO LOOT //========================= function autoLoot(){ if( !AutoBattle.autoLoot )return; for( const drop of Drops ){ if( Math.hypot( drop.x- Player.x, drop.y- Player.y )<100 ){ pickupDrop( drop ); } } } //========================= // MAIN //========================= function updateAutoBattle(dt){ if( !AutoBattle.enable )return; autoFindTarget(); autoMove(dt); autoAttack(); autoSkill(); autoPotion(); autoLoot(); } //========================= // UI //========================= function drawAutoBattle(){ ctx.fillStyle= AutoBattle.enable? "#00ff66": "#ff3333"; ctx.font="18px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Auto : "+ ( AutoBattle.enable? "ON": "OFF" ), 20, 30 ); if( AutoBattle.target ){ ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.fillText( "Target : "+ AutoBattle.target.name, 20, 55 ); } } //========================= // AUTO FARM //========================= function autoFarm(){ if( !AutoBattle.enable )return; if( AutoBattle.target )return; const angle= Math.random()* Math.PI*2; Player.x+= Math.cos(angle)*80; Player.y+= Math.sin(angle)*80; } //========================= // SKILL COMBO SYSTEM //========================= const ComboSystem={ combo:0, timer:0, maxTime:3, multiplier:1, history:[], unlocks:[] }; //========================= // ADD COMBO //========================= function addCombo(skillId){ ComboSystem.combo++; ComboSystem.timer= ComboSystem.maxTime; ComboSystem.history.push( skillId ); if( ComboSystem.history .length>6 ){ ComboSystem.history.shift(); } updateComboBonus(); checkSkillCombo(); } //========================= // BONUS //========================= function updateComboBonus(){ ComboSystem.multiplier= 1+ Math.min( ComboSystem.combo, 100 )*0.01; } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateCombo(dt){ if( ComboSystem.timer>0 ){ ComboSystem.timer-=dt; } else{ ComboSystem.combo=0; ComboSystem.multiplier=1; ComboSystem.history=[]; } } //========================= // DAMAGE //========================= function comboDamage( damage ){ return Math.floor( damage* ComboSystem .multiplier ); } //========================= // SPECIAL COMBO //========================= function checkSkillCombo(){ const h= ComboSystem.history; if( h.length<3 )return; const last= h.slice(-3) .join("-"); if( last== "FireSlash-WindSlash-ThunderStrike" ){ triggerComboSkill( "Storm Break" ); } if( last== "IceShot-IceShot-Blizzard" ){ triggerComboSkill( "Frozen World" ); } if( last== "Dash-Slash-Uppercut" ){ triggerComboSkill( "Dragon Combo" ); } } //========================= // SPECIAL SKILL //========================= function triggerComboSkill( name ){ ComboSystem.unlocks.push({ name, time:2 }); for( const enemy of Enemies ){ if( enemy.dead )continue; const d= Math.hypot( enemy.x- Player.x, enemy.y- Player.y ); if( d<250 ){ enemy.hp-= Player.attack*6; } } } //========================= // EFFECT //========================= function updateComboEffects(dt){ for( let i= ComboSystem .unlocks .length-1; i>=0; i-- ){ ComboSystem .unlocks[i] .time-=dt; if( ComboSystem .unlocks[i] .time<=0 ){ ComboSystem .unlocks .splice(i,1); } } } //========================= // DRAW //========================= function drawComboUI(){ if( ComboSystem.combo<=0 )return; ctx.fillStyle="gold"; ctx.font="26px Arial"; ctx.fillText( ComboSystem.combo+ " COMBO", canvas.width/2-70, 50 ); ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.font="18px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "x"+ ComboSystem .multiplier .toFixed(2), canvas.width/2-15, 75 ); let y=105; for( const s of ComboSystem .unlocks ){ ctx.fillStyle="#00ffff"; ctx.fillText( s.name, canvas.width/2-50, y ); y+=22; } } //========================= // RANK //========================= function comboRank(){ if( ComboSystem.combo>=150 )return"S"; if( ComboSystem.combo>=100 )return"A"; if( ComboSystem.combo>=60 )return"B"; if( ComboSystem.combo>=30 )return"C"; return"D"; } //========================= // REWARD //========================= function comboReward(){ if( ComboSystem.combo<50 )return; Player.exp+= ComboSystem.combo*2; Player.gold+= ComboSystem.combo; } //========================= // RESET //========================= function resetCombo(){ ComboSystem.combo=0; ComboSystem.timer=0; ComboSystem.multiplier=1; ComboSystem.history=[]; } //========================= // QUEST ENGINE //========================= const QuestSystem={ open:false, quests:[], active:[], completed:[], daily:[], weekly:[] }; //========================= // QUEST //========================= class Quest{ constructor(data){ this.id=data.id; this.name=data.name; this.type=data.type; this.level=data.level||1; this.npc=data.npc||""; this.description=data.description||""; this.target=data.target||""; this.require=data.require||0; this.progress=0; this.finished=false; this.reward=data.reward||{ gold:0, exp:0 }; this.next=data.next||null; } } //========================= // CREATE //========================= function createQuest(data){ QuestSystem.quests.push( new Quest(data) ); } //========================= // ACCEPT //========================= function acceptQuest(id){ const q= QuestSystem.quests.find( a=>a.id==id ); if(!q)return; if( QuestSystem.active.includes(q) )return; QuestSystem.active.push(q); } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateQuestProgress( target, value=1 ){ for( const q of QuestSystem.active ){ if( q.finished )continue; if( q.target!=target )continue; q.progress+=value; if( q.progress>= q.require ){ q.progress= q.require; q.finished=true; } } } //========================= // COMPLETE //========================= function completeQuest(id){ const index= QuestSystem.active.findIndex( q=>q.id==id ); if( index==-1 )return; const q= QuestSystem.active[index]; if( !q.finished )return; Player.gold+= q.reward.gold; Player.exp+= q.reward.exp; QuestSystem.completed.push(q); QuestSystem.active.splice( index, 1 ); if( q.next ){ acceptQuest( q.next ); } } //========================= // NPC TALK //========================= function npcQuestTalk(npc){ for( const q of QuestSystem.quests ){ if( q.npc==npc&& !QuestSystem .completed .includes(q) ){ acceptQuest( q.id ); break; } } } //========================= // DRAW //========================= function drawQuestUI(){ if( !QuestSystem.open )return; ctx.fillStyle= "rgba(0,0,0,.92)"; ctx.fillRect( 80, 40, 860, 620 ); ctx.strokeStyle="#66ccff"; ctx.strokeRect( 80, 40, 860, 620 ); ctx.fillStyle="gold"; ctx.font="28px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Quest Journal", 120, 80 ); let y=130; ctx.font="18px Arial"; for( const q of QuestSystem.active ){ ctx.fillStyle= q.finished? "#00ff55": "white"; ctx.fillText( q.name, 120, y ); ctx.fillText( q.progress+ "/"+ q.require, 420, y ); ctx.fillText( q.type, 560, y ); y+=30; } } //========================= // HOTKEY //========================= window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if( e.key.toLowerCase()=="q" ){ QuestSystem.open= !QuestSystem.open; } }); //========================= // DEFAULT QUESTS //========================= createQuest({ id:"q001", name:"Slime Trouble", type:"Main", npc:"Village Chief", description: "Defeat Slimes.", target:"Slime", require:10, reward:{ gold:500, exp:250 }, next:"q002" }); createQuest({ id:"q002", name:"Goblin Camp", type:"Main", npc:"Village Chief", description: "Defeat Goblins.", target:"Goblin", require:20, reward:{ gold:1200, exp:600 }, next:"q003" }); createQuest({ id:"q003", name:"Wolf Hunt", type:"Main", npc:"Hunter", description: "Hunt Wolves.", target:"Wolf", require:25, reward:{ gold:2500, exp:1500 } }); //========================= // DAILY QUEST //========================= function generateDailyQuest(){ QuestSystem.daily=[]; QuestSystem.daily.push( new Quest({ id: "daily_"+ Date.now(), name: "Daily Monster Hunt", type:"Daily", target:"Any", require:100, reward:{ gold:10000, exp:8000 } }) ); } //========================= // WEEKLY QUEST //========================= function generateWeeklyQuest(){ QuestSystem.weekly=[]; QuestSystem.weekly.push( new Quest({ id: "weekly_"+ Date.now(), name: "Weekly Boss Raid", type:"Weekly", target:"Boss", require:20, reward:{ gold:100000, exp:70000 } }) ); } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateQuestSystem(){ if( QuestSystem.daily .length==0 ){ generateDailyQuest(); } if( QuestSystem.weekly .length==0 ){ generateWeeklyQuest(); } } //========================= // NPC DIALOG SYSTEM //========================= const DialogueSystem={ open:false, npc:null, page:0, text:[], options:[], typing:0, speed:40 }; //========================= // NPC CLASS //========================= class NPCDialogue{ constructor( name, dialog, options=[] ){ this.name=name; this.dialog=dialog; this.options=options; } } //========================= // DATABASE //========================= const DialogueDB={ }; //========================= // REGISTER //========================= function registerDialogue( npc, dialog ){ DialogueDB[npc]=dialog; } //========================= // OPEN //========================= function openDialogue( npc ){ const d= DialogueDB[npc]; if(!d)return; DialogueSystem.open=true; DialogueSystem.npc=npc; DialogueSystem.page=0; DialogueSystem.text= d.dialog; DialogueSystem.options= d.options; DialogueSystem.typing=0; } //========================= // CLOSE //========================= function closeDialogue(){ DialogueSystem.open=false; } //========================= // NEXT //========================= function nextDialogue(){ DialogueSystem.page++; DialogueSystem.typing=0; if( DialogueSystem.page>= DialogueSystem.text.length ){ closeDialogue(); } } //========================= // TYPE EFFECT //========================= function updateDialogue(dt){ if( !DialogueSystem.open )return; DialogueSystem.typing+= dt* DialogueSystem.speed; } //========================= // DRAW //========================= function drawDialogue(){ if( !DialogueSystem.open )return; ctx.fillStyle= "rgba(0,0,0,.88)"; ctx.fillRect( 70, 470, 900, 180 ); ctx.strokeStyle="#66ccff"; ctx.strokeRect( 70, 470, 900, 180 ); ctx.fillStyle="gold"; ctx.font="24px Arial"; ctx.fillText( DialogueSystem.npc, 95, 505 ); ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.font="20px Arial"; const line= DialogueSystem.text[ DialogueSystem.page ]|| ""; ctx.fillText( line.substring( 0, DialogueSystem.typing ), 95, 550 ); let y=600; for( let i=0; i< DialogueSystem .options .length; i++ ){ ctx.fillStyle="#00ff99"; ctx.fillText( ( i+1 )+ ". "+ DialogueSystem .options[i], 100, y ); y+=25; } } //========================= // OPTION //========================= function selectDialogueOption( index ){ const txt= DialogueSystem .options[index]; if( txt== "Accept Quest" ){ npcQuestTalk( DialogueSystem.npc ); } if( txt== "Shop" ){ Shop.open=true; } if( txt== "Goodbye" ){ closeDialogue(); } } //========================= // INPUT //========================= window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if( !DialogueSystem.open )return; if( e.key==" " ){ nextDialogue(); } const n= parseInt( e.key ); if( !isNaN(n) ){ selectDialogueOption( n-1 ); } }); //========================= // SAMPLE NPC //========================= registerDialogue( "Village Chief", new NPCDialogue( "Village Chief", [ "Our village is under attack.", "Please help defeat the Slimes.", "Return after finishing the mission." ], [ "Accept Quest", "Goodbye" ] ) ); registerDialogue( "Hunter", new NPCDialogue( "Hunter", [ "The forest is dangerous.", "Wolves are everywhere.", "Can you hunt them?" ], [ "Accept Quest", "Shop", "Goodbye" ] ) ); //========================= // TALK //========================= function interactNPC( npc ){ openDialogue( npc.name ); } //========================= // STORY CUTSCENE ENGINE //========================= const CutsceneSystem={ playing:false, scene:null, step:0, timer:0, wait:3, fade:0, mode:"fadein" }; //========================= // CUTSCENE //========================= class Cutscene{ constructor( id, events ){ this.id=id; this.events=events; } } const CutsceneDB={}; //========================= // REGISTER //========================= function registerCutscene( id, events ){ CutsceneDB[id]= new Cutscene( id, events ); } //========================= // START //========================= function startCutscene( id ){ const scene= CutsceneDB[id]; if(!scene)return; CutsceneSystem.playing=true; CutsceneSystem.scene=scene; CutsceneSystem.step=0; CutsceneSystem.timer=0; CutsceneSystem.fade=1; Player.lock=true; } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateCutscene(dt){ if( !CutsceneSystem.playing )return; CutsceneSystem.timer+=dt; const event= CutsceneSystem .scene .events[ CutsceneSystem.step ]; if(!event)return; if( CutsceneSystem.timer>= event.time ){ executeCutscene( event ); CutsceneSystem.timer=0; CutsceneSystem.step++; if( CutsceneSystem.step>= CutsceneSystem .scene .events.length ){ endCutscene(); } } } //========================= // EXECUTE //========================= function executeCutscene( event ){ switch( event.type ){ case"dialog": openDialogue( event.npc ); break; case"move": Player.x= event.x; Player.y= event.y; break; case"camera": Camera.target= event.target; break; case"spawn": spawnEnemy( event.enemy, event.x, event.y ); break; case"music": playMusic( event.name ); break; } } //========================= // END //========================= function endCutscene(){ CutsceneSystem.playing=false; Player.lock=false; } //========================= // DRAW //========================= function drawCutscene(){ if( !CutsceneSystem.playing )return; ctx.fillStyle= "rgba(0,0,0,.35)"; ctx.fillRect( 0, 0, canvas.width, 90 ); ctx.fillRect( 0, canvas.height-90, canvas.width, 90 ); } //========================= // SAMPLE //========================= registerCutscene( "intro", [ { type:"music", name:"opening", time:0 }, { type:"dialog", npc:"Village Chief", time:3 }, { type:"move", x:450, y:330, time:2 }, { type:"spawn", enemy:"Goblin King", x:600, y:350, time:2 } ] ); //========================= // QUEST EVENT //========================= function checkStoryQuest(){ if( QuestSystem .completed .find( q=>q.id=="q003" ) ){ if( !GameFlags .introBoss ){ GameFlags .introBoss=true; startCutscene( "intro" ); } } } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateStory(){ updateCutscene( deltaTime ); checkStoryQuest(); } //========================= // WORLD EVENT ENGINE //========================= const WorldEvent={ active:null, timer:0, duration:900, cooldown:1800, events:[], bossSpawned:false }; //========================= // EVENT //========================= class EventData{ constructor( id, name, type ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.type=type; this.running=false; } } //========================= // REGISTER //========================= function registerWorldEvent( id, name, type ){ WorldEvent.events.push( new EventData( id, name, type ) ); } //========================= // EVENTS //========================= registerWorldEvent( "meteor", "Meteor Shower", "Battle" ); registerWorldEvent( "invasion", "Goblin Invasion", "Battle" ); registerWorldEvent( "dragon", "Ancient Dragon", "Raid" ); registerWorldEvent( "treasure", "Treasure Rain", "Reward" ); registerWorldEvent( "harvest", "Harvest Festival", "Peace" ); //========================= // START //========================= function startWorldEvent(){ if( WorldEvent.active )return; const event= WorldEvent.events[ Math.floor( Math.random()* WorldEvent.events.length ) ]; WorldEvent.active=event; event.running=true; WorldEvent.timer= WorldEvent.duration; eventStartAction( event ); } //========================= // ACTION //========================= function eventStartAction( event ){ switch( event.id ){ case"invasion": for( let i=0; i<40; i++ ){ spawnEnemy( "Goblin", Math.random()*3000, Math.random()*3000 ); } break; case"dragon": if( !WorldEvent .bossSpawned ){ spawnBoss( "Ancient Dragon", 1500, 1500 ); WorldEvent .bossSpawned=true; } break; case"treasure": for( let i=0; i<150; i++ ){ spawnDrop( generateEquipment( Player.level ), Math.random()*3000, Math.random()*3000 ); } break; } } //========================= // END //========================= function endWorldEvent(){ if( !WorldEvent.active )return; WorldEvent.active.running=false; WorldEvent.active=null; WorldEvent.timer=0; WorldEvent.bossSpawned=false; } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateWorldEvent(dt){ if( WorldEvent.active ){ WorldEvent.timer-=dt; if( WorldEvent.timer<=0 ){ endWorldEvent(); } } else{ WorldEvent.cooldown-=dt; if( WorldEvent.cooldown<=0 ){ WorldEvent.cooldown= 1800; startWorldEvent(); } } } //========================= // DRAW //========================= function drawWorldEvent(){ if( !WorldEvent.active )return; ctx.fillStyle= "#ffcc00"; ctx.font= "28px Arial"; ctx.fillText( WorldEvent .active .name, canvas.width/2-120, 40 ); ctx.font= "18px Arial"; ctx.fillText( Math.floor( WorldEvent.timer )+" s", canvas.width/2-20, 65 ); } //========================= // REWARD //========================= function worldEventReward(){ if( !WorldEvent.active )return; Player.gold+= 5000; Player.exp+= 3000; } //========================= // PARTICIPATION //========================= Player.eventPoint=0; function addEventPoint(v){ Player.eventPoint+=v; } //========================= // RANK //========================= function getEventRank(){ if( Player.eventPoint> 1000 )return"S"; if( Player.eventPoint> 700 )return"A"; if( Player.eventPoint> 400 )return"B"; return"C"; } //========================= // BOSS AI ENGINE //========================= const BossAI={ phase:1, skillTimer:0, rage:false, summonTimer:20 }; //========================= // INIT //========================= function initBossAI( boss ){ boss.phase=1; boss.skillCooldown=3; boss.rage=false; boss.target=Player; } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateBossAI( boss, dt ){ if( boss.dead )return; updateBossPhase( boss ); boss.skillCooldown-=dt; if( boss.skillCooldown<=0 ){ bossUseSkill( boss ); boss.skillCooldown= 4+ Math.random()*2; } bossMove( boss, dt ); } //========================= // PHASE //========================= function updateBossPhase( boss ){ const hp= boss.hp/ boss.maxHp; if( hp<=0.7&& boss.phase==1 ){ boss.phase=2; boss.attack*=1.2; boss.moveSpeed*=1.1; } if( hp<=0.4&& boss.phase==2 ){ boss.phase=3; boss.attack*=1.3; boss.moveSpeed*=1.2; boss.rage=true; } } //========================= // MOVE //========================= function bossMove( boss, dt ){ const dx= Player.x- boss.x; const dy= Player.y- boss.y; const dist= Math.hypot( dx, dy ); if( dist>120 ){ boss.x+= dx/dist* boss.moveSpeed* dt; boss.y+= dy/dist* boss.moveSpeed* dt; } } //========================= // SKILLS //========================= function bossUseSkill( boss ){ const list=[ bossFireball, bossMeteor, bossShockwave, bossSummon, bossLaser ]; const skill= list[ Math.floor( Math.random()* list.length ) ]; skill( boss ); } //========================= // FIREBALL //========================= function bossFireball( boss ){ spawnProjectile({ x:boss.x, y:boss.y, tx:Player.x, ty:Player.y, damage: boss.attack*1.5, speed:320, sprite:"fireball" }); } //========================= // METEOR //========================= function bossMeteor( boss ){ for( let i=0; i<6; i++ ){ spawnMeteor( Player.x+ Math.random()*300-150, Player.y+ Math.random()*300-150, boss.attack*2 ); } } //========================= // SHOCKWAVE //========================= function bossShockwave( boss ){ for( const p of Players ){ if( Math.hypot( p.x-boss.x, p.y-boss.y )<220 ){ p.hp-= boss.attack; } } } //========================= // SUMMON //========================= function bossSummon( boss ){ for( let i=0; i<4; i++ ){ spawnEnemy( "Elite Goblin", boss.x+ Math.random()*250-125, boss.y+ Math.random()*250-125 ); } } //========================= // LASER //========================= function bossLaser( boss ){ createLaser({ x:boss.x, y:boss.y, angle: Math.atan2( Player.y-boss.y, Player.x-boss.x ), length:700, damage: boss.attack*2.5 }); } //========================= // DRAW PHASE //========================= function drawBossPhase( boss ){ ctx.fillStyle="red"; ctx.font="20px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Phase "+ boss.phase, boss.x-35, boss.y-60 ); if( boss.rage ){ ctx.fillStyle="#ff00ff"; ctx.fillText( "RAGE", boss.x-25, boss.y-82 ); } } //========================= // NPC SCHEDULE ENGINE //========================= const NPCSchedule={ dayLength:1440, currentTime:480, speed:6, list:[] }; //========================= // NPC DAILY ROUTINE //========================= class DailyNPC{ constructor( npc ){ this.npc=npc; this.schedule=[]; this.currentTask=null; } } //========================= // REGISTER //========================= function registerNPCSchedule( npc ){ const d= new DailyNPC( npc ); NPCSchedule.list.push( d ); return d; } //========================= // ADD TASK //========================= function addNPCTask( daily, start, end, x, y, action ){ daily.schedule.push({ start, end, x, y, action }); } //========================= // UPDATE TIME //========================= function updateWorldClock( dt ){ NPCSchedule.currentTime+= dt* NPCSchedule.speed; if( NPCSchedule.currentTime>= NPCSchedule.dayLength ){ NPCSchedule.currentTime=0; } } //========================= // NPC AI //========================= function updateNPCSchedule( dt ){ for( const daily of NPCSchedule.list ){ for( const job of daily.schedule ){ if( NPCSchedule.currentTime>= job.start&& NPCSchedule.currentTime< job.end ){ daily.currentTask= job; npcWalk( daily.npc, job.x, job.y, dt ); } } } } //========================= // MOVE //========================= function npcWalk( npc, tx, ty, dt ){ const dx= tx- npc.x; const dy= ty- npc.y; const dist= Math.hypot( dx, dy ); if( dist<4 )return; npc.x+= dx/dist* npc.speed* dt; npc.y+= dy/dist* npc.speed* dt; } //========================= // DRAW CLOCK //========================= function drawClock(){ const hour= Math.floor( NPCSchedule.currentTime/ 60 ); const minute= Math.floor( NPCSchedule.currentTime% 60 ); ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.font="20px Arial"; ctx.fillText( String(hour) .padStart(2,"0") +":" + String(minute) .padStart(2,"0"), canvas.width-120, 30 ); } //========================= // SAMPLE NPC //========================= const chiefRoutine= registerNPCSchedule( VillageChief ); addNPCTask( chiefRoutine, 360, 480, 320, 210, "Wake" ); addNPCTask( chiefRoutine, 480, 720, 430, 260, "Office" ); addNPCTask( chiefRoutine, 720, 780, 360, 340, "Lunch" ); addNPCTask( chiefRoutine, 780, 1080, 430, 260, "Work" ); addNPCTask( chiefRoutine, 1080, 1320, 520, 430, "Home" ); addNPCTask( chiefRoutine, 1320, 1440, 300, 200, "Sleep" ); //========================= // SHOP OPEN //========================= function isShopOpen(){ return( NPCSchedule .currentTime>= 480&& NPCSchedule .currentTime<= 1200 ); } //========================= // NPC DIALOG //========================= function npcGreeting( npc ){ const t= NPCSchedule .currentTime; if( t<720 )return "Good morning!"; if( t<1080 )return "Good afternoon!"; return "Good evening!"; } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateNPCWorld( dt ){ updateWorldClock( dt ); updateNPCSchedule( dt ); } //========================= // WEATHER ENGINE //========================= const WeatherSystem={ current:"Sunny", timer:600, duration:600, particles:[], list:[ "Sunny", "Cloudy", "Rain", "Storm", "Snow", "Fog" ] }; //========================= // CHANGE WEATHER //========================= function changeWeather(){ WeatherSystem.current= WeatherSystem.list[ Math.floor( Math.random()* WeatherSystem.list.length ) ]; WeatherSystem.timer= WeatherSystem.duration; WeatherSystem.particles=[]; initWeatherParticles(); } //========================= // PARTICLE //========================= class WeatherParticle{ constructor(){ this.x= Math.random()* World.width; this.y= Math.random()* World.height; this.speed= 150+ Math.random()*250; this.size= 2+ Math.random()*3; } } //========================= // INIT //========================= function initWeatherParticles(){ let amount=0; switch( WeatherSystem.current ){ case"Rain": amount=250; break; case"Snow": amount=180; break; case"Storm": amount=350; break; case"Fog": amount=120; break; } for( let i=0; i World.height ){ p.y=0; p.x= Math.random()* World.width; } } } //========================= // EFFECT //========================= function updateWeatherBonus(){ switch( WeatherSystem.current ){ case"Rain": Player.moveSpeed*=0.96; break; case"Storm": Player.attack*=1.08; break; case"Snow": Player.moveSpeed*=0.92; break; case"Fog": Camera.zoom=0.9; break; case"Sunny": Player.hpRegen+=2; break; } } //========================= // DRAW //========================= function drawWeather(){ switch( WeatherSystem.current ){ case"Rain": ctx.strokeStyle= "#66AAFF"; for( const p of WeatherSystem .particles ){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo( p.x, p.y ); ctx.lineTo( p.x-3, p.y+10 ); ctx.stroke(); } break; case"Snow": ctx.fillStyle= "#FFFFFF"; for( const p of WeatherSystem .particles ){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc( p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI*2 ); ctx.fill(); } break; case"Fog": ctx.fillStyle= "rgba(220,220,220,.15)"; ctx.fillRect( 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height ); break; } } //========================= // UI //========================= function drawWeatherUI(){ ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.font="18px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Weather : "+ WeatherSystem.current, canvas.width-220, 60 ); } //========================= // BOSS BONUS //========================= function weatherBossBonus( boss ){ if( WeatherSystem.current== "Storm" ){ boss.attack*=1.10; } if( WeatherSystem.current== "Snow" ){ boss.moveSpeed*=0.9; } } //========================= // START //========================= changeWeather(); //========================= // MAP STREAMING ENGINE //========================= const MapStreaming={ chunkSize:512, radius:2, loaded:new Map(), currentChunkX:0, currentChunkY:0 }; //========================= // CHUNK //========================= class Chunk{ constructor( x, y ){ this.x=x; this.y=y; this.id=x+"_"+y; this.loaded=false; this.objects=[]; this.monsters=[]; this.npcs=[]; this.resources=[]; } } //========================= // LOAD //========================= function loadChunk( cx, cy ){ const id= cx+"_"+cy; if( MapStreaming .loaded .has(id) )return; const chunk= new Chunk( cx, cy ); generateChunk( chunk ); chunk.loaded=true; MapStreaming .loaded .set( id, chunk ); } //========================= // UNLOAD //========================= function unloadChunk( cx, cy ){ const id= cx+"_"+cy; if( !MapStreaming .loaded .has(id) )return; const chunk= MapStreaming .loaded .get(id); saveChunk( chunk ); MapStreaming .loaded .delete(id); } //========================= // GENERATE //========================= function generateChunk( chunk ){ for( let i=0; i<20; i++ ){ chunk.monsters.push( spawnEnemy( "Slime", chunk.x* MapStreaming .chunkSize+ Math.random()* MapStreaming .chunkSize, chunk.y* MapStreaming .chunkSize+ Math.random()* MapStreaming .chunkSize ) ); } for( let i=0; i<6; i++ ){ chunk.resources.push({ type:"Tree", x: chunk.x* MapStreaming .chunkSize+ Math.random()* MapStreaming .chunkSize, y: chunk.y* MapStreaming .chunkSize+ Math.random()* MapStreaming .chunkSize }); } } //========================= // SAVE //========================= function saveChunk( chunk ){ // Reserved for Save System } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateStreaming(){ const cx= Math.floor( Player.x/ MapStreaming .chunkSize ); const cy= Math.floor( Player.y/ MapStreaming .chunkSize ); if( cx== MapStreaming .currentChunkX&& cy== MapStreaming .currentChunkY )return; MapStreaming .currentChunkX=cx; MapStreaming .currentChunkY=cy; for( let x= cx- MapStreaming .radius; x<= cx+ MapStreaming .radius; x++ ){ for( let y= cy- MapStreaming .radius; y<= cy+ MapStreaming .radius; y++ ){ loadChunk( x, y ); } } for( const [ id, chunk ] of MapStreaming .loaded ){ if( Math.abs( chunk.x-cx )> MapStreaming .radius|| Math.abs( chunk.y-cy )> MapStreaming .radius ){ unloadChunk( chunk.x, chunk.y ); } } } //========================= // MINIMAP //========================= function drawLoadedChunks(){ const size=16; let ox= canvas.width-180; let oy=90; for( const [ id, chunk ] of MapStreaming .loaded ){ ctx.fillStyle= "#44ff88"; ctx.fillRect( ox+ ( chunk.x- MapStreaming .currentChunkX+ 2 )*size, oy+ ( chunk.y- MapStreaming .currentChunkY+ 2 )*size, size-2, size-2 ); } } //========================= // DEBUG //========================= function drawChunkBorder(){ ctx.strokeStyle= "rgba(0,255,255,.25)"; for( const [ id, chunk ] of MapStreaming .loaded ){ ctx.strokeRect( chunk.x* MapStreaming .chunkSize- Camera.x, chunk.y* MapStreaming .chunkSize- Camera.y, MapStreaming .chunkSize, MapStreaming .chunkSize ); } } //========================= // START //========================= updateStreaming(); //========================= // BIOME ENGINE //========================= const BiomeSystem={ current:"Grassland", list:{}, discovered:new Set() }; //========================= // BIOME //========================= class Biome{ constructor( name, color ){ this.name=name; this.color=color; this.monsters=[]; this.resources=[]; this.music=""; this.weather=[]; this.levelMin=1; this.levelMax=999; } } //========================= // REGISTER //========================= function registerBiome( name, color ){ const biome= new Biome( name, color ); BiomeSystem.list[name]= biome; return biome; } //========================= // DEFAULT BIOMES //========================= const grass= registerBiome( "Grassland", "#44bb44" ); grass.monsters=[ "Slime", "Wolf", "Rabbit" ]; grass.resources=[ "Tree", "Flower", "Stone" ]; grass.weather=[ "Sunny", "Rain" ]; grass.music="field_theme"; grass.levelMin=1; grass.levelMax=15; const forest= registerBiome( "Forest", "#226622" ); forest.monsters=[ "Goblin", "Spider", "Boar" ]; forest.resources=[ "Wood", "Herb", "Mushroom" ]; forest.weather=[ "Rain", "Fog" ]; forest.music="forest_theme"; forest.levelMin=10; forest.levelMax=30; const desert= registerBiome( "Desert", "#D6B15A" ); desert.monsters=[ "Scorpion", "Mummy", "Sand Worm" ]; desert.resources=[ "Cactus", "Crystal", "Ore" ]; desert.weather=[ "Sunny" ]; desert.music="desert_theme"; desert.levelMin=30; desert.levelMax=50; const snow= registerBiome( "Snow Land", "#EAF6FF" ); snow.monsters=[ "Yeti", "Ice Wolf", "Snow Golem" ]; snow.resources=[ "Ice", "Snow Herb" ]; snow.weather=[ "Snow" ]; snow.music="snow_theme"; snow.levelMin=50; snow.levelMax=80; //========================= // GET BIOME //========================= function getBiomeAt( cx, cy ){ if( cy<3 )return grass; if( cx>4&& cy<5 )return forest; if( cx>7 )return desert; return snow; } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateBiome(){ const cx= Math.floor( Player.x/ MapStreaming.chunkSize ); const cy= Math.floor( Player.y/ MapStreaming.chunkSize ); const biome= getBiomeAt( cx, cy ); if( biome.name== BiomeSystem.current )return; BiomeSystem.current= biome.name; BiomeSystem .discovered .add( biome.name ); WeatherSystem.list= biome.weather; playMusic( biome.music ); } //========================= // CHUNK GENERATOR //========================= function generateBiomeChunk( chunk ){ const biome= getBiomeAt( chunk.x, chunk.y ); for( let i=0; i<25; i++ ){ const monster= biome.monsters[ Math.floor( Math.random()* biome.monsters.length ) ]; chunk.monsters.push( spawnEnemy( monster, chunk.x* MapStreaming.chunkSize+ Math.random()* MapStreaming.chunkSize, chunk.y* MapStreaming.chunkSize+ Math.random()* MapStreaming.chunkSize ) ); } for( let i=0; i<15; i++ ){ const resource= biome.resources[ Math.floor( Math.random()* biome.resources.length ) ]; chunk.resources.push({ type:resource, x:chunk.x* MapStreaming.chunkSize+ Math.random()* MapStreaming.chunkSize, y:chunk.y* MapStreaming.chunkSize+ Math.random()* MapStreaming.chunkSize }); } } //========================= // UI //========================= function drawBiomeUI(){ ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.font="22px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Biome : "+ BiomeSystem.current, canvas.width-240, 90 ); } //========================= // BONUS //========================= function biomePlayerBonus(){ switch( BiomeSystem.current ){ case"Grassland": Player.hpRegen=3; break; case"Forest": Player.critRate+=2; break; case"Desert": Player.fireResist+=10; break; case"Snow Land": Player.iceResist+=15; break; } } //========================= // DISCOVERY //========================= function drawDiscovery(){ ctx.fillStyle="#00ffff"; ctx.font="16px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Discovered : "+ BiomeSystem .discovered .size+ "/20", canvas.width-220, 120 ); } //========================= // KINGDOM BUILDING SYSTEM //========================= const Kingdom={ level:1, exp:0, gold:0, population:15, happiness:100, defense:50, food:500, wood:500, stone:500, iron:200, buildings:[] }; //========================= // BUILDING //========================= class Building{ constructor( name, type, level ){ this.id= Date.now()+ Math.random(); this.name=name; this.type=type; this.level=level; this.hp=1000; this.maxHp=1000; this.worker=0; this.production=1; } } //========================= // CREATE //========================= function createBuilding( name, type ){ const b= new Building( name, type, 1 ); break; } } } //========================= // UPGRADE //========================= function upgradeBuilding( id ){ const b= Kingdom.buildings.find( a=>a.id==id ); if(!b)return; const cost= b.level*300; if( Kingdom.wooda.id==id ); if(!b)return; b.hp=b.maxHp; } //========================= // POPULATION //========================= function updatePopulation(){ Kingdom.population+= Math.random()*0.02; if( Kingdom.food< Kingdom.population ){ Kingdom.happiness-= 0.1; } } //========================= // LEVEL //========================= function updateKingdomLevel(){ if( Kingdom.exp>= Kingdom.level* 10000 ){ Kingdom.exp=0; Kingdom.level++; Kingdom.population+=20; } } //========================= // DRAW UI //========================= function drawKingdomUI(){ ctx.fillStyle= "rgba(0,0,0,.85)"; ctx.font="18px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Level : "+Kingdom.level, 60, 110 ); ctx.fillText( "Population : "+ Math.floor( Kingdom.population ), 60, 140 ); ctx.fillText( "Food : "+ Math.floor( Kingdom.food ), 60, 170 ); ctx.fillText( "Wood : "+ Math.floor( Kingdom.wood ), 60, 200 ); ctx.fillText( "Stone : "+ Math.floor( Kingdom.stone ), 60, 230 ); ctx.fillText( "Iron : "+ Math.floor( Kingdom.iron ), 60, 260 ); ctx.fillText( "Defense : "+ Math.floor( Kingdom.defense ), 60, 290 ); } //========================= // HOTKEY //========================= window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if( e.key=="K"|| e.key=="k" ){ UI.kingdom= !UI.kingdom; } }); //========================= // MAIN //========================= function updateKingdomSystem(dt){ updateKingdom(dt); } //========================= // OBJECT //========================= function spawnGuildWarObjects(){ GuildWar.towers=[]; GuildWar.crystals=[]; for( let i=0; i<4; i++ ){ GuildWar.towers.push({ hp:80000, x:900+ i*220, y:600 }); } if( GuildWar .castle .captureProgress>=100 ){ GuildWar .castle .owner= Player.guild; } } //========================= // TOWER //========================= function damageTower( index, damage ){ const tower= GuildWar .towers[index]; crystal.hp-=damage; if( crystal.hp<=0 ){ GuildWar.scoreA+= 5000; finishGuildWar(); } } //========================= // FINISH //========================= function finishGuildWar(){ GuildWar.active=false; GuildWar.state="Finished"; guildWarReward(); } //========================= // REWARD //========================= function guildWarReward(){ if( GuildWar.scoreA> GuildWar.scoreB ){ Player.gold+= 500000; //========================= // FLOOR //========================= class TowerFloor{ constructor( level ){ this.level=level; this.clear=false; this.rewardGold= level*1500; this.rewardExp= level*2500; } } //========================= // DATABASE //========================= const TowerFloors=[]; ctx.fillText( "Highest : "+ TowerSystem .highestFloor, canvas.width/2-45, 90 ); ctx.fillText( "Time : "+ Math.floor( TowerSystem.timer ), canvas.width/2-35, 115 ); ctx.fillText( "Rank : "+ towerRank(), canvas.width/2-35, 140 ); } //========================= // BLESSING //========================= function towerBlessing(){ if( TowerSystem.floor% 25==0 ){ Player.attack+=8; Player.maxHp+=80; Player.defense+=5; } } //========================= // HOTKEY //========================= window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if( e.key=="F12" ){ enterTower(); } }); //========================= // CHAOS DUNGEON ENGINE //========================= const ChaosDungeon={ active:false, level:1, difficulty:1, corruption:0, timer:900, portal:null, boss:null, modifiers:[], rewardMultiplier:1 }; //========================= // MODIFIER //========================= class ChaosModifier{ constructor( name, effect ){ this.name=name; this.effect=effect; } } const ChaosModifiers=[ new ChaosModifier( "Double Monster HP", ()=>{ EnemyBonus.hp*=2; } }); //========================= // MARKETPLACE ENGINE //========================= const Marketplace={ listings:[], history:[], taxRate:0.05, maxListing:1000 }; //========================= // LISTING //========================= class MarketListing{ constructor( seller, item, price, amount ){ this.id= crypto.randomUUID(); //========================= // AWAKEN DATA //========================= class AwakeningTier{ constructor( id, name, levelNeed ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.levelNeed=levelNeed; this.attack=0; this.hp=0; this.skill=""; } } const AwakeningData=[]; //========================= // REGISTER //========================= function registerAwakening( id, name, level, atk, hp, skill ){ const a= new AwakeningTier( id, name, level ); a.attack=atk; a.hp=hp; a.skill=skill; AwakeningData.push( a ); } //========================= // TIERS //========================= registerAwakening( 1, "Wind Knight", 50, 100, 1000, "Wind Slash" ); registerAwakening( 2, "Wind Lord", 120, 300, 3000, "Hurricane" ); //========================= // AWAKEN //========================= function awakenPlayer(){ const next= AwakeningData[ AwakeningSystem.tier ]; if( !next )return; if( Player.level< next.levelNeed )return; AwakeningSystem.tier++; Player.attack+= next.attack; Player.maxHp+= next.hp; AwakeningSystem .unlockedSkills .push( next.skill ); AwakeningSystem.aura= next.name; } //========================= // TRANSCEND //========================= function transcendPlayer(){ if( Player.level<500 )return; } //========================= // BONUS //========================= function updateAwakeningBonus(){ for( const p of AwakeningSystem .passiveBonus ){ switch( p.name ){ case"Attack": Player.attack+= p.value; ctx.fillStyle= "white"; ctx.font= "18px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Tier : "+ AwakeningSystem.tier, 130, 120 ); ctx.fillText( "Aura : "+ ( AwakeningSystem.aura|| "None" ), 130, 150 ); ctx.fillText( "Transcend : "+ AwakeningSystem .transcendLevel, 130, 180 ); let y=240; for( const skill of AwakeningSystem .unlockedSkills ){ ctx.fillText( skill, 150, y ); y+=26; } } //========================= // HOTKEY //========================= window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if( e.key=="Y"|| e.key=="y" ){ UI.awakening= !UI.awakening; } }); //========================= // DEFAULT PASSIVE //========================= learnPassive( "Attack", 50 ); learnPassive( "HP", 500 ); learnPassive( "Crit", 3 ); //========================= // COMPANION AI V2 ENGINE //========================= const CompanionSystem={ companions:[], maxParty:4, formation:"Diamond", mode:"Follow", sharedExp:true }; //========================= // COMPANION //========================= class Companion{ constructor( name, role ){ this.id= crypto.randomUUID(); this.name=name; this.role=role; this.level=1; this.hp=1000; this.maxHp=1000; this.mp=300; this.attack=80; this.defense=40; this.range=180; this.followDistance=80; this.target=null; this.state="Follow"; this.skillCooldown=0; this.friendship=0; this.dead=false; } } //========================= // CREATE //========================= function addCompanion( name, role ){ if( CompanionSystem .companions .length>= CompanionSystem .maxParty )return; CompanionSystem .companions .push( new Companion( name, role ) ); } //========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateCompanions(dt){ for( const c of CompanionSystem .companions ){ if( c.dead )continue; updateCompanionAI( c, dt ); } } //========================= // AI //========================= function updateCompanionAI( c, dt ){ c.skillCooldown-=dt; c.target= findNearestEnemy( c.x, c.y, 500 ); switch( c.role ){ case"Tank": tankAI( c, dt ); break; case"DPS": dpsAI( c, dt ); break; case"Healer": healerAI( c, dt ); break; case"Support": supportAI( c, dt ); break; } } //========================= // TANK //========================= function tankAI( c, dt ){ if( c.target ){ moveToTarget( c, c.target, dt ); attackEnemy( c, c.target ); } } //========================= // DPS //========================= function dpsAI( c, dt ){ if( c.target ){ keepDistance( c, c.target, 180, dt ); attackEnemy( c, c.target ); } } //========================= // HEALER //========================= function healerAI( c, dt ){ const ally= lowestHpParty(); if( ally&& c.skillCooldown<=0 ){ ally.hp+= 300; c.skillCooldown=8; createHealEffect( ally.x, ally.y ); } followPlayer( c, dt ); } //========================= // SUPPORT //========================= function supportAI( c, dt ){ if( c.skillCooldown<=0 ){ Player.attack+=20; Player.moveSpeed+=5; c.skillCooldown=12; } followPlayer( c, dt ); } //========================= // FOLLOW //========================= function followPlayer( c, dt ){ const dx= Player.x-c.x; const dy= Player.y-c.y; const d= Math.hypot( dx, dy ); if( d> c.followDistance ){ c.x+= dx/d* 220* dt; c.y+= dy/d* 220* dt; } } //========================= // FRIENDSHIP //========================= function addFriendship( c, value ){ c.friendship+=value; if( c.friendship>=100 ){ c.attack+=20; c.maxHp+=300; } } //========================= // REVIVE //========================= function reviveCompanion( c ){ c.dead=false; c.hp= c.maxHp*0.5; } //========================= // UI //========================= function drawCompanionParty(){ let y=120; for( const c of CompanionSystem .companions ){ ctx.fillStyle="#222"; ctx.fillRect( 20, y, 220, 50 ); ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.font="16px Arial"; ctx.fillText( c.name, 30, y+20 ); ctx.fillText( c.role, 150, y+20 ); ctx.fillStyle="red"; ctx.fillRect( 30, y+30, c.hp/ c.maxHp* 170, 8 ); y+=60; } } //========================= // DEFAULT PARTY //========================= addCompanion( "Luna", "Healer" ); addCompanion( "Leon", "Tank" ); addCompanion( "Iris", "DPS" ); addCompanion( "Sage", "Support" ); //========================= // PERFORMANCE ENGINE V1 // QuadTree + Object Pool //========================= const Performance={ quadTree:null, pools:{}, visibleObjects:[], activeEffects:[], maxEffects:500, fps:60 }; //========================= // RECT //========================= class Rect{ constructor( x, y, w, h ){ this.x=x; this.y=y; this.w=w; this.h=h; } contains(obj){ return( obj.x>=this.x&& obj.x<=this.x+this.w&& obj.y>=this.y&& obj.y<=this.y+this.h ); } intersects(range){ return !( range.x> this.x+this.w|| range.x+range.w< this.x|| range.y> this.y+this.h|| range.y+ range.h< this.y ); } } //========================= // QUADTREE //========================= class QuadTree{ constructor( boundary, capacity ){ this.boundary= boundary; this.capacity= capacity; this.points=[]; this.divided=false; } subdivide(){ const x= this.boundary.x; const y= this.boundary.y; const w= this.boundary.w/2; const h= this.boundary.h/2; this.northWest= new QuadTree( new Rect( x, y, w, h ), this.capacity ); this.northEast= new QuadTree( new Rect( x+w, y, w, h ), this.capacity ); this.southWest= new QuadTree( new Rect( x, y+h, w, h ), this.capacity ); this.southEast= new QuadTree( new Rect( x+w, y+h, w, h ), this.capacity ); this.divided=true; } insert(obj){ if( !this.boundary .contains(obj) )return false; if( this.points.length< this.capacity ){ this.points.push( obj ); return true; } if( !this.divided ){ this.subdivide(); } return( this.northWest.insert(obj)|| this.northEast.insert(obj)|| this.southWest.insert(obj)|| this.southEast.insert(obj) ); } query( range, found=[] ){ if( !this.boundary .intersects( range ) ){ return found; } for( const p of this.points ){ if( range.contains( p ) ){ found.push( p ); } } if( this.divided ){ this.northWest.query( range, found ); this.northEast.query( range, found ); this.southWest.query( range, found ); this.southEast.query( range, found ); } return found; } } //========================= // BUILD //========================= function rebuildQuadTree(){ Performance .quadTree= new QuadTree( new Rect( 0, 0, World.width, World.height ), 8 ); for( const e of Enemies ){ Performance .quadTree .insert(e); } } //========================= // VISIBLE //========================= function updateVisibleObjects(){ const range= new Rect( Camera.x, Camera.y, canvas.width, canvas.height ); Performance .visibleObjects= Performance .quadTree .query(range); } //========================= // OBJECT POOL //========================= class ObjectPool{ constructor( create, size ){ this.pool=[]; for( let i=0; i({ x:0, y:0, life:0, active:false }), 800 ); //========================= // SPAWN EFFECT //========================= function spawnEffect( x, y ){ const fx= Performance .pools .effect .obtain(); if( !fx )return; fx.x=x; fx.y=y; fx.life=1; fx.active=true; Performance .activeEffects .push(fx); } //========================= // UPDATE FX //========================= function updateEffects(dt){ for( let i= Performance .activeEffects .length-1; i>=0; i-- ){ const fx= Performance .activeEffects[i]; fx.life-=dt; if( fx.life<=0 ){ fx.active=false; Performance .activeEffects .splice( i, 1 ); Performance .pools .effect .release(fx); } } } //========================= // FPS //========================= let fpsFrame=0; let fpsTime=0; function updateFPS(dt){ fpsFrame++; fpsTime+=dt; if( fpsTime>=1 ){ Performance.fps= fpsFrame; fpsFrame=0; fpsTime=0; } } //========================= // DEBUG //========================= function drawPerformanceUI(){ ctx.fillStyle="#00FF00"; ctx.font="16px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "FPS : "+ Performance.fps, 20, 25 ); ctx.fillText( "Visible : "+ Performance .visibleObjects .length, 20, 45 ); ctx.fillText( "Effects : "+ Performance .activeEffects .length, 20, 65 ); } //========================= // SAVE / LOAD SYSTEM V3 //========================= const SaveSystem={ slot:1, version:"3.0.0", autoSave:120, timer:0 }; //========================= // BUILD DATA //========================= function buildSaveData(){ return{ version: SaveSystem.version, time: Date.now(), player:{ name:Player.name, level:Player.level, exp:Player.exp, gold:Player.gold, hp:Player.hp, maxHp:Player.maxHp, mp:Player.mp, maxMp:Player.maxMp, attack:Player.attack, defense:Player.defense, x:Player.x, y:Player.y, map:World.currentMap }, inventory: Inventory.items, equipment: Equipment.slots, skills: SkillBar, pets: PetSystem.pets, companions: CompanionSystem.companions, quests:{ active: QuestSystem.active, completed: QuestSystem.completed }, collection:{ monsters: Array.from( CollectionSystem .monsters ), bosses: Array.from( CollectionSystem .bosses ), items: Array.from( CollectionSystem .items ) }, kingdom: Kingdom, guild: GuildSystem.guild, awakening: AwakeningSystem, relics: RelicSystem, tower:{ floor: TowerSystem.floor, highest: TowerSystem .highestFloor }, settings: GameSettings }; } //========================= // SAVE //========================= function saveGame(){ const data= buildSaveData(); localStorage.setItem( "hero_save_"+ SaveSystem.slot, JSON.stringify(data) ); showMessage( "Game Saved" ); } //========================= // LOAD //========================= function loadGame(){ const raw= localStorage.getItem( "hero_save_"+ SaveSystem.slot ); if(!raw)return; const data= JSON.parse(raw); applySaveData(data); TowerSystem.highestFloor= data.tower.highest; } } //========================= // AUTO SAVE //========================= function updateAutoSave(dt){ SaveSystem.timer+=dt; if( SaveSystem.timer>= SaveSystem.autoSave ){ SaveSystem.timer=0; } //========================= // DELETE //========================= function deleteSave(){ localStorage.removeItem( "hero_save_"+ SaveSystem.slot ); } //========================= // HOTKEY //========================= window.addEventListener( "keydown", e=>{ if(e.key=="F5"){ e.preventDefault(); //========================= // WINDOW //========================= class UIWindow{ constructor( id, title, x, y, w, h ){ this.id=id; this.title=title; this.x=x; this.y=y; this.width=w; this.height=h; this.visible=true; this.minimized=false; this.locked=false; this.alpha=0; } } //========================= // CREATE //========================= function createWindow( id, title, x, y, w, h ){ const win= new UIWindow( id, title, x, y, w, h ); } //========================= // WORLD MAP GENERATOR V2 // Infinite Streaming World //========================= const WorldGenerator={ chunkSize:64, renderDistance:4, loadedChunks:new Map(), seed:938421, biomeNoise:[], objectNoise:[] }; //========================= // CHUNK //========================= class WorldChunk{ constructor( cx, cy ){ this.cx=cx; this.cy=cy; this.tiles=[]; this.objects=[]; this.loaded=false; } } //========================= // NOISE //========================= function worldNoise( x, y, seed ){ const n= Math.sin( x*127.1+ y*311.7+ seed )* 43758.5453; return n- Math.floor( n ); } //========================= // LOAD //========================= function loadChunk( cx, cy ){ const key= cx+"_"+cy; if( WorldGenerator .loadedChunks .has(key) ){ return; } const chunk= new WorldChunk( cx, cy ); generateChunk( chunk ); WorldGenerator .loadedChunks .set( key, chunk ); } //========================= // GENERATE //========================= function generateChunk( chunk ){ for( let y=0; y< WorldGenerator .chunkSize; y++ ){ chunk.tiles[y]=[]; for( let x=0; x< WorldGenerator .chunkSize; x++ ){ const wx= chunk.cx* WorldGenerator .chunkSize+x; const wy= chunk.cy* WorldGenerator .chunkSize+y; const n= worldNoise( wx, wy, WorldGenerator.seed ); let tile= "grass"; if( n<0.18 ) tile="water"; else if( n<0.28 ) tile="sand"; else if( n<0.70 ) tile="grass"; else if( n<0.86 ) tile="forest"; else tile="mountain"; chunk.tiles[y][x]= tile; generateWorldObject( chunk, tile, wx, wy ); } } chunk.loaded=true; } //========================= // OBJECT //========================= function generateWorldObject( chunk, tile, x, y ){ const r= worldNoise( x, y, 999 ); if( tile=="forest"&& r>0.96 ){ chunk.objects.push({ type:"Tree", x, y }); } if( tile=="grass"&& r>0.992 ){ chunk.objects.push({ type:"Flower", x, y }); } if( tile=="mountain"&& r>0.985 ){ chunk.objects.push({ type:"Ore", x, y }); } } //========================= // STREAM //========================= function updateChunkStreaming(){ const pcx= Math.floor( Player.x/ WorldGenerator .chunkSize ); const pcy= Math.floor( Player.y/ WorldGenerator .chunkSize ); for( let y= -WorldGenerator .renderDistance; y<= WorldGenerator .renderDistance; y++ ){ for( let x= -WorldGenerator .renderDistance; x<= WorldGenerator .renderDistance; x++ ){ loadChunk( pcx+x, pcy+y ); } } unloadFarChunks( pcx, pcy ); } //========================= // UNLOAD //========================= function unloadFarChunks( pcx, pcy ){ for( const [ key, chunk ] of WorldGenerator .loadedChunks ){ if( Math.abs( chunk.cx- pcx )> WorldGenerator .renderDistance+1|| Math.abs( chunk.cy- pcy )> WorldGenerator .renderDistance+1 ){ WorldGenerator .loadedChunks .delete( key ); } } } //========================= // DRAW //========================= function drawChunks(){ for( const chunk of WorldGenerator .loadedChunks .values() ){ drawChunk( chunk ); } } //========================= // DEBUG //========================= function drawChunkInfo(){ ctx.fillStyle="#00FFAA"; ctx.font="16px Arial"; ctx.fillText( "Chunks : "+ WorldGenerator .loadedChunks .size, 20, 90 ); } //========================= // SAVE //========================= function serializeChunks(){ return Array.from( WorldGenerator .loadedChunks .values() ); } //========================= // NPC DAILY LIFE ENGINE V2 // Living World Simulation //========================= const NPCLifeSystem={ npcs:[], schedules:new Map(), relationships:new Map(), houses:new Map(), workplaces:new Map(), festivals:[] }; //========================= // NPC //========================= class LivingNPC{ constructor( id, name, job ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.job=job; this.x=0; this.y=0; this.home=null; this.work=null; this.state="Idle"; this.energy=100; this.happiness=100; this.friendship=0; this.currentTask=null; this.path=[]; this.inventory=[]; } } //========================= // CREATE NPC //========================= function createLivingNPC( name, job ){ const npc= new LivingNPC( crypto.randomUUID(), name, job ); } switch( npc.currentTask ){ case"Sleep": npc.state="Sleeping"; npc.energy+= dt*5; break; case"Work": npc.state="Working"; moveToWork( npc, dt ); break; case"Eat": npc.state="Eating"; npc.energy+= dt; break; case"Walk": npc.state="Walking"; randomWalk( npc, dt ); break; case"Shop": npc.state="Shopping"; moveToShop( npc, dt ); break; } } //========================= // TALK //========================= function talkNPC( npc ){ npc.friendship++; if( npc.friendship% 20==0 ){ giveNPCGift( npc ); } } //========================= // GIFT //========================= function giveNPCGift( npc ){ Player.gold+=500; npc.happiness+=5; } //========================= // FESTIVAL //========================= function startFestival( name ){ NPCLifeSystem .festivals .push({ name, active:true }); //========================= // WORLD BOSS //========================= class WorldBoss{ constructor( id, name, biome, level ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.biome=biome; this.level=level; this.maxHp=1000000; if( !boss|| !boss.spawned )return; boss.hp-=damage; const old= WorldBossSystem .damageLog .get(playerId)||0; WorldBossSystem .damageLog .set( playerId, old+damage ); updateBossPhase( boss ); if( boss.hp<=0 ){ defeatWorldBoss( boss ); } } //========================= // PHASE //========================= function updateBossPhase( boss ){ const percent= boss.hp/ boss.maxHp; if( percent<0.75 )boss.phase=2; if( percent<0.50 )boss.phase=3; if( percent<0.25 )boss.phase=4; } //========================= // DEFEAT //========================= function defeatWorldBoss( boss ){ boss.spawned=false; distributeBossReward( boss ); WorldBossSystem .damageLog .clear(); WorldBossSystem .activeBoss=null; } //========================= // REWARD //========================= function distributeBossReward( boss ){ const ranking= [...WorldBossSystem .damageLog .entries()] .sort( (a,b)=> b[1]-a[1] ); for( let i=0; i DungeonSystem .current .floors ){ completeDungeon(); return; } generateDungeonFloor(); } //========================= // COMPLETE //========================= function completeDungeon(){ Inventory.add({ name: randomChoice( DungeonSystem .current .rewards ) }); Player.exp+= 10000; Player.gold+= 50000; DungeonSystem .current=null; } //========================= // BOSS //========================= function isBossFloor(){ return( DungeonSystem .floor% DungeonSystem .current .bossEvery==0 ); } //========================= // BOSS SPAWN //========================= function spawnDungeonBoss(){ spawnMonsterById( randomChoice( DungeonSystem .current .bosses ), 1200, 900 ); } //========================= // UI //========================= function drawDungeonHUD(){ if( !DungeonSystem .current )return; } //========================= // LEVEL //========================= function classLevelUp(){ const cls= ClassSystem .currentClass; if( !cls )return; Player.maxHp+= cls.growth.hp||0; Player.maxMp+= cls.growth.mp||0; Player.attack+= cls.growth.atk||0; Player.defense+= cls.growth.def||0; Player.critRate+= cls.growth.crit||0; } //========================= // ADVANCE //========================= function advanceClass( newClass ){ ClassSystem .subclass= newClass; ctx.fillText( ClassSystem .currentClass .name, 20, 260 ); } //========================= // TALENT TREE SYSTEM V2 //========================= const TalentSystem={ trees:new Map(), unlocked:new Map(), points:0 }; //========================= // TALENT //========================= class TalentNode{ constructor( id, name ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.maxLevel=5; this.level=0; this.cost=1; this.require=[]; this.effect=null; this.position={ x:0, y:0 }; } } //========================= // TREE //========================= class TalentTree{ constructor( classId ){ this.classId=classId; this.nodes=[]; } } //========================= // CREATE //========================= function createTalentTree( classId ){ const tree= new TalentTree( classId ); TalentSystem .trees .set( classId, tree ); return tree; } //========================= // ADD NODE //========================= function addTalentNode( tree, id, name, x, y, effect ){ const node= new TalentNode( id, name ); } ); n2.require.push( "atk1" ); n3.require.push( "hp1" ); //========================= // LEVEL //========================= function learnTalent( treeId, nodeId ){ const tree= TalentSystem .trees .get( treeId ); if( !tree )return; const node= tree.nodes.find( n=>n.id==nodeId ); if( !node )return; if( node.level>= node.maxLevel )return; if( TalentSystem .points< node.cost )return; for( const req of node.require ){ const r= tree.nodes.find( n=>n.id==req ); if( !r|| r.level==0 )return; } TalentSystem .points-= node.cost; node.level++; node.effect(); } //========================= // RESET //========================= function resetTalentTree( treeId ){ const tree= TalentSystem .trees .get( treeId ); if( !tree )return; for( const node of tree.nodes ){ TalentSystem.points+= node.level; node.level=0; } recalculatePlayerStats(); } //========================= // LEVEL UP //========================= function gainTalentPoint(){ TalentSystem .points++; } //========================= // DRAW //========================= function drawTalentTree(){ const tree= TalentSystem .trees .get( ClassSystem .currentClass .id ); if( !tree )return; for( const node of tree.nodes ){ ctx.fillStyle= node.level>0? "#44DD66": "#555"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc( node.position.x, node.position.y, 24, 0, Math.PI*2 ); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle= "#FFF"; ctx.font= "14px Arial"; ctx.fillText( node.name, node.position.x-35, node.position.y+45 ); } } //========================= // AUTO SAVE //========================= function exportTalentTree(){ const data={}; for( const [ id, tree ] of TalentSystem .trees ){ data[id]= tree.nodes.map( n=> n.level ); } } } //========================= // EQUIPMENT SET SYSTEM V2 //========================= const SetSystem={ sets:new Map(), active:new Map(), resonance:0 }; //========================= // SET //========================= class EquipmentSet{ constructor( id, name ){ this.id=id;
