//========================= // UPDATE //========================= function updateEquipmentSet(){ SetSystem .active.clear(); for( const set of SetSystem .sets .values() ){ let count=0; for( const item of set.items ){ if( Equipment.has( item ) ){ count++; } } if( count>0 ){ SetSystem .active .set( set.id, count ); applySetBonus( set, count ); } } } //========================= // APPLY //========================= function applySetBonus( set, count ){ for( const need in set.bonus ){ if( count>=need ){ const bonus= set.bonus[need]; applyBonus( bonus ); } } } //========================= // BONUS //========================= function applyBonus( bonus ){ if( bonus.attack ) Player.attack+= bonus.attack; if( bonus.hp ) Player.maxHp+= bonus.hp; if( bonus.crit ) Player.critRate+= bonus.crit; if( bonus.speed ) Player.moveSpeed+= bonus.speed; if( bonus.skillDamage ) Player.skillDamage+= bonus.skillDamage; if( bonus.unlockSkill ){ unlockSkill( bonus.unlockSkill ); } } //========================= // UI //========================= function drawEquipmentSet(){ let y=260; for( const [ id, count ] of SetSystem .active ){ const set= SetSystem .sets .get(id); ctx.fillStyle= "#FFD700"; ctx.font= "16px Arial"; ctx.fillText( set.name+ " ("+ count+ "/6)", 940, y ); y+=22; } } //========================= // SAVE //========================= function exportSetData(){ return{ resonance: SetSystem .resonance }; } function importSetData( data ){ SetSystem .resonance= data.resonance; } //========================= // RUNE BOARD SYSTEM V1 // End Game Build System //========================= const RuneBoard={ nodes:new Map(), links:[], activated:new Set(), points:0, keystones:new Map() }; //========================= // NODE //========================= class RuneNode{ constructor( id, name, type ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.type=type; this.cost=1; this.position={ x:0, y:0 }; this.connected=[]; this.effect=null; this.active=false; } } //========================= // CREATE //========================= function createRuneNode( id, name, type, x, y, effect ){ const node= new RuneNode( id, name, type ); node.position.x=x; node.position.y=y; node.effect=effect; RuneBoard .nodes .set( id, node ); return node; } //========================= // CONNECT //========================= function connectRune( a, b ){ RuneBoard .nodes .get(a) .connected .push(b); RuneBoard .nodes .get(b) .connected .push(a); RuneBoard .links .push([ a, b ]); } //========================= // BASIC //========================= createRuneNode( "start", "Origin", "Core", 450, 350, ()=>{} ); } ); createRuneNode( "speed01", "Wind Step", "Rare", 760, 500, ()=>{ Player.moveSpeed+=5; } ); //========================= // KEYSTONE //========================= createRuneNode( "berserk", "Berserker Soul", "Keystone", 980, 420, ()=>{ Player.attack*=1.40; Player.defense*=0.70; } ); RuneBoard .keystones .set( "berserk", true ); //========================= // LINKS //========================= connectRune( "start", "atk01" ); connectRune( "atk01", "hp01" ); connectRune( "atk01", "crit01" ); connectRune( "crit01", "speed01" ); connectRune( "speed01", "berserk" ); //========================= // ACTIVATE //========================= function activateRune( id ){ const node= RuneBoard .nodes .get(id); if( !node|| node.active|| RuneBoard.points< node.cost )return; node.active=true; RuneBoard.points-= node.cost; RuneBoard .activated .add(id); if( node.effect ){ node.effect(); } } //========================= // RESET //========================= function resetRuneBoard(){ RuneBoard.points+= RuneBoard .activated .size; for( const node of RuneBoard .nodes .values() ){ node.active=false; } RuneBoard .activated .clear(); recalculatePlayerStats(); } //========================= // DRAW //========================= function drawRuneBoard(){ ctx.lineWidth=3; for( const link of RuneBoard .links ){ const a= RuneBoard .nodes .get(link[0]); const b= RuneBoard .nodes .get(link[1]); ctx.strokeStyle= "#666"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo( a.position.x, a.position.y ); ctx.lineTo( b.position.x, b.position.y ); ctx.stroke(); } for( const node of RuneBoard .nodes .values() ){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc( node.position.x, node.position.y, node.type=="Keystone"?28:18, 0, Math.PI*2 ); ctx.fillStyle= node.active? "#33FF88": "#555"; ctx.fill(); } } //========================= // SAVE //========================= function exportRuneBoard(){ return{ points: RuneBoard.points, active:[ ...RuneBoard .activated ] }; } function importRuneBoard( data ){ RuneBoard.points= data.points; for( const id of data.active ){ activateRune( id ); } } //========================= // ARTIFACT RESONANCE SYSTEM //========================= const ArtifactSystem={ artifacts:new Map(), equipped:[], resonanceLevel:0, resonanceEffects:[], blessing:0 }; //========================= // ARTIFACT //========================= class Artifact{ constructor( id, name, rarity ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.rarity=rarity; this.level=1; this.maxLevel=50; this.exp=0; this.stats={}; this.skills=[]; this.tags=[]; this.icon=""; } } //========================= // REGISTER //========================= function registerArtifact( id, name, rarity ){ const art= new Artifact( id, name, rarity ); ArtifactSystem .artifacts .set( id, art ); return art; } //========================= // SAMPLE //========================= const windCore= registerArtifact( "wind_core", "Core of Wind", "Mythic" ); windCore.stats={ attack:180, crit:8, speed:12 }; windCore.skills.push( "Wind Burst" ); windCore.tags.push( "Wind", "Ancient" ); const dragonHeart= registerArtifact( "dragon_heart", "Dragon Heart", "Ancient" ); dragonHeart.stats={ hp:3500, attack:260, fireDamage:15 }; //========================= // SEASON DATABASE //========================= const Seasons=[ { name:"Spring", weather:[ "Sunny", "Rain", "Wind" ], effects:{ farming:1.2, monster:0.9 } }, { name:"Summer", weather:[ "Sunny", "Heat", "Storm" ], effects:{ farming:1.5, monster:1.1 } }, { name:"Autumn", weather:[ "Wind", "Fog", "Rain" ], effects:{ farming:1.1, monster:1.2 } }, { name:"Winter", weather:[ "Snow", "Blizzard", "Fog" ], effects:{ farming:0.5, monster:1.5 } } ]; //========================= // GET SEASON //========================= function getCurrentSeason(){ return Seasons[ ClimateSystem.season ]; } //========================= // TIME SYSTEM //========================= function updateTime(dt){ ClimateSystem.minute+=dt; break; } } //========================= // LIGHTNING EVENT //========================= function spawnLightning(){ let x= Player.x+ (Math.random()*400-200); let y= Player.y+ (Math.random()*400-200); createEffect( x, y, "lightning" ); } //========================= // DAY NIGHT SYSTEM //========================= function getTimePhase(){ let h= ClimateSystem.hour; if( h>=6&&h<12 ) return"Morning"; if( h>=12&&h<18 ) return"Day"; if( h>=18&&h<22 ) return"Evening"; this.hp=10000; this.maxHp=10000; this.attack=500; this.defense=200; this.phase=1; this.skills=[]; this.enraged=false; this.rewards=[]; } } //================================================ // CREATE BOSS //================================================ function createBoss( id, name, level ){ let boss= new WorldBoss( id, name, level ); windDragon.rewards=[ "Dragon Wing", "Ancient Gem" ]; //================================================ // BOSS PHASE SYSTEM //================================================ function updateBossPhase( boss ){ let percent= boss.hp/ boss.maxHp; if(percent<0.5){ boss.phase=2; boss.attack*=1.5; } if(percent<0.2){ boss.phase=3; boss.enraged=true; boss.attack*=2; } } //================================================ // RAID EVENT //================================================ const RaidSystem={ active:false, players:[], boss:null }; function startRaidBoss( bossId ){ let boss= BossSystem .bosses .get( bossId ); if(!boss) return; RaidSystem.active=true; RaidSystem.boss=boss; } function finishRaid(){ if(!RaidSystem.boss) return; for( let reward of RaidSystem.boss.rewards ){ Inventory.add({ name:reward }); } RaidSystem.active=false; } //================================================ // DRAW WILDLIFE //================================================ function drawWildlife(){ for( let a of WildlifeSystem.active ){ ctx.fillStyle= a.type=="Aggressive"? "brown": "green"; ctx.fillRect( a.x-8, a.y-8, 16, 16 ); } } //================================================ // WEATHER & SEASON SYSTEM V7 //================================================ const ClimateSystem={ hour:8, day:1, month:1, year:1, season:"Spring", weather:"Clear", temperature:25, weatherTimer:0, events:[] }; //================================================ // WEATHER DATABASE //================================================ const WeatherTypes={ CLEAR:"Clear", RAIN:"Rain", STORM:"Storm", SNOW:"Snow", FOG:"Fog", SANDSTORM:"Sandstorm", HEAT:"Heat Wave" }; //================================================ // SEASON DATABASE //================================================ const Seasons={ Spring:{ temperature:20, weather:[ "Clear", "Rain", "Fog" ] }, Summer:{ temperature:35, weather:[ "Clear", "Heat Wave", "Storm" ] }, Autumn:{ temperature:18, weather:[ "Clear", "Rain" ] }, Winter:{ temperature:5, weather:[ "Snow", "Storm", "Fog" ] } }; //================================================ // UPDATE TIME //================================================ function updateClimate(dt){ ClimateSystem.hour+= dt*0.02; if( ClimateSystem.hour>=24 ){ ClimateSystem.hour=0; nextDay(); } ClimateSystem.weatherTimer-=dt; if( ClimateSystem.weatherTimer<=0 ){ changeWeather(); } } //================================================ // DAY SYSTEM //================================================ function nextDay(){ ClimateSystem.day++; if( ClimateSystem.day>30 ){ ClimateSystem.day=1; ClimateSystem.month++; } if( ClimateSystem.month>12 ){ ClimateSystem.month=1; ClimateSystem.year++; } updateSeason(); } //================================================ // SEASON CHANGE //================================================ function updateSeason(){ let m= ClimateSystem.month; if(m>=3&&m<=5) ClimateSystem.season="Spring"; else if(m>=6&&m<=8) ClimateSystem.season="Summer"; else if(m>=9&&m<=11) ClimateSystem.season="Autumn"; else ClimateSystem.season="Winter"; ClimateSystem.temperature= Seasons [ ClimateSystem.season ] .temperature; } //================================================ // WEATHER CHANGE //================================================ function changeWeather(){ let season= Seasons [ ClimateSystem.season ]; let list= season.weather; ClimateSystem.weather= list[ Math.floor( Math.random()*list.length ) ]; ClimateSystem.weatherTimer= 300; applyWeatherEffect(); } //================================================ // WEATHER EFFECT //================================================ function applyWeatherEffect(){ switch( ClimateSystem.weather ){ case"Rain": Player.moveSpeed*=0.95; break; case"Storm": Player.moveSpeed*=0.9; MonsterSystem.active.forEach( m=>{ m.attack*=1.1; } ); break; case"Snow": Player.moveSpeed*=0.9; break; case"Heat Wave": Player.hp-=1; break; case"Sandstorm": Player.attack*=0.95; break; } } //================================================ // ENVIRONMENT EFFECT //================================================ const EnvironmentSystem={ effects:new Map() }; function registerEnvironment( id, data ){ EnvironmentSystem .effects .set( id, data ); } registerEnvironment( "forest", { hpRegen:2, moveBonus:5 } ); registerEnvironment( "desert", { temperature:40, staminaLoss:1 } ); registerEnvironment( "ice", { temperature:-20, movePenalty:10 } ); //================================================ // TEMPERATURE SYSTEM //================================================ function updateTemperature(){ let biome= WorldMapSystem.currentBiome; if(!biome) return; switch( biome.type ){ case"Desert": ClimateSystem.temperature+=0.1; break; case"Snow": ClimateSystem.temperature-=0.1; break; } } //================================================ // DYNAMIC WORLD EVENT //================================================ const WorldEventSystem={ events:new Map(), active:null }; //================================================ // EVENT CLASS //================================================ class WorldEvent{ constructor( id, name ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.duration=600; this.rewards=[]; this.active=false; } } function createWorldEvent( id, name ){ let event= new WorldEvent( id, name ); WorldEventSystem .events .set( id, event ); return event; } const dragonAttack= createWorldEvent( "dragon_attack", "Dragon Army Invasion" ); if(!event) return; event.duration-=dt; if( event.duration<=0 ){ event.active=false; WorldEventSystem.active=null; } } //================================================ // WEATHER DRAW //================================================ function drawWeather(){ switch( ClimateSystem.weather ){ case"Rain": ctx.fillStyle="rgba(50,100,255,.3)"; for(let i=0;i<100;i++){ ctx.fillRect( Math.random()*canvas.width, Math.random()*canvas.height, 2, 10 ); } break; case"Fog": ctx.fillStyle="rgba(200,200,200,.2)"; ctx.fillRect( 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height ); break; case"Snow": ctx.fillStyle="white"; for(let i=0;i<80;i++){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc( Math.random()*canvas.width, Math.random()*canvas.height, 3, 0, Math.PI*2 ); ctx.fill(); } break; } } //================================================ // SAVE CLIMATE //================================================ function exportClimateData(){ return{ day: ClimateSystem.day, month: ClimateSystem.month, season: ClimateSystem.season, weather: ClimateSystem.weather }; } function importClimateData(data){ if(!data) return; ClimateSystem.day=data.day; ClimateSystem.month=data.month; ClimateSystem.season=data.season; ClimateSystem.weather=data.weather; } //================================================ // LIFE SKILL & ECONOMY SYSTEM V8 // FARMING - FISHING - MINING //================================================ const LifeSkillSystem={ farming:{level:1,exp:0}, fishing:{level:1,exp:0}, mining:{level:1,exp:0}, crafting:{level:1,exp:0} }; //================================================ // FARMING SYSTEM //================================================ const FarmingSystem={ fields:[], crops:new Map(), plants:[] }; //================================================ // CROP CLASS //================================================ class Crop{ constructor( id, name, growTime, reward ){ this.id=id; //================================================ // PLANT //================================================ function plantCrop( id, x, y ){ let base= FarmingSystem .crops .get( id ); if(!base) return; let crop= Object.assign( new Crop(), JSON.parse( JSON.stringify(base) ) ); crop.x=x; crop.y=y; FarmingSystem .plants .push( crop ); } //================================================ // UPDATE FARM //================================================ function updateFarm(dt){ for( let plant of FarmingSystem.plants ){ if( plant.ready ) continue; plant.time+=dt; if( plant.time>=plant.growTime ){ plant.ready=true; } } } //================================================ // HARVEST //================================================ function harvestCrop( plant ){ if(!plant.ready) return; let result; if(chance<0.05) result="dragon_fish"; else if(chance<0.3) result="gold_fish"; else result="small_fish"; let fish= FishingSystem .fish .get( result ); Inventory.add({ name:fish.name }); gainLifeExp( "fishing", 15 ); } //================================================ // MINING SYSTEM //================================================ const MiningSystem={ ores:new Map(), nodes:[] }; class Ore{ constructor( id, name, rarity ){ this.id=id; } function importLifeSkill(data){ if(!data) return; Object.assign( LifeSkillSystem, data ); } //================================================ // ADVANCED CRAFTING SYSTEM V9 // FORGE - ALCHEMY - COOKING - HOUSING //================================================ const AdvancedCraftSystem={ forges:new Map(), recipes:new Map(), alchemy:new Map(), food:new Map() }; return rep; } createReputation( "wind_kingdom", "Wind Kingdom" ); createReputation( "dragon_clan", "Dragon Clan" ); function addReputation( id, amount ){ let rep= ReputationSystem .regions .get( id ); //================================================ // GUILD CLASS //================================================ class Guild{ constructor( id, name ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.level=1; this.exp=0; this.maxMembers=20; this.members=[]; this.leader=null; this.gold=0; this.buildings=[]; this.quests=[]; this.rank="Bronze"; } } //================================================ // CREATE GUILD //================================================ function createGuild( id, name, leader ){ let guild= new Guild( id, name ); if(!guild) return; guild.exp+=amount; while( guild.exp>=1000 ){ guild.exp-=1000; guild.level++; guild.maxMembers+=5; updateGuildRank( guild ); } } //================================================ // GUILD RANK //================================================ function updateGuildRank( guild ){ if( guild.level>=50 ) guild.rank="Legendary"; else if( guild.level>=20 ) guild.rank="Gold"; else if( guild.level>=10 ) guild.rank="Silver"; else guild.rank="Bronze"; } //================================================ // GUILD BUILDING //================================================ class GuildBuilding{ constructor( id, name, level ){ this.id=id; } buildGuildBuilding( WindGuardians, "guild_hall", "Guild Hall" ); buildGuildBuilding( WindGuardians, "training_room", "Training Room" ); //================================================ // GUILD QUEST //================================================ class GuildQuest{ constructor( id, name, target ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.target=target; this.progress=0; this.reward=[]; } } function createGuildQuest( id, name, target ){ return new GuildQuest( id, name, target ); } WindGuardians.quests.push( createGuildQuest( "monster_hunt", "Defeat Monsters", 100 ) ); function updateGuildQuest( type ){ let guild= GuildSystem.current; if(!guild) return; for( let quest of guild.quests ){ if( quest.id==type ){ quest.progress++; } } } //================================================ // TERRITORY SYSTEM //================================================ class Territory{ constructor( id, name ){ this.id=id; return territory; } createTerritory( "forest_land", "Emerald Forest Territory" ); createTerritory( "dragon_valley", "Dragon Valley" ); //================================================ // CLAIM TERRITORY //================================================ function claimTerritory( guildId, territoryId ){ let guild= GuildSystem .guilds .get( guildId ); let territory= GuildSystem .territories .get( territoryId ); if( !guild|| !territory ) return; territory.owner= guild; territory.defense+=100; } //================================================ // GUILD WAR //================================================ class GuildWar{ constructor( guildA, guildB ){ this.guildA=guildA; this.guildB=guildB; this.duration=3600; this.scoreA=0; this.scoreB=0; this.active=true; } } function startGuildWar( guildA, guildB ){ let war= new GuildWar( guildA, guildB ); GuildSystem .wars .push( war ); return war; } function updateGuildWar(dt){ for( let war of GuildSystem.wars ){ if(!war.active) continue; war.duration-=dt; if( war.duration<=0 ){ war.active=false; } } } //================================================ // GUILD BONUS //================================================ function applyGuildBonus(){ let guild= GuildSystem.current; if(!guild) return; Player.maxHp+= guild.level*10; Player.attack+= guild.level*2; } //================================================ // SAVE GUILD //================================================ function exportGuildData(){ return{ guild: GuildSystem.current? GuildSystem.current.id:null, level: GuildSystem.current? GuildSystem.current.level:0 }; } //================================================ // ARENA PVP SYSTEM V12 // RANKING - SEASON - TOURNAMENT //================================================ const ArenaSystem={ players:new Map(), matches:[], ranking:[], season:1, seasonReward:[] }; //================================================ // PVP PLAYER DATA //================================================ class ArenaPlayer{ constructor( id, name ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.rating=1000; this.rank="Bronze"; this.wins=0; this.losses=0; this.kill=0; this.death=0; this.battlePower=0; } } //================================================ // REGISTER ARENA PLAYER //================================================ function registerArenaPlayer( id, name ){ let player= new ArenaPlayer( id, name ); ArenaSystem .players .set( id, player ); return player; } const HeroArena= registerArenaPlayer( "hero", "Hero" ); //================================================ // BATTLE POWER //================================================ function calculateBattlePower(){ let power=0; power+=Player.attack*10; power+=Player.defense*5; power+=Player.maxHp/10; if( EquipmentSystem.current ){ power+=500; } return power; } function updateArenaPower( arenaPlayer ){ arenaPlayer.battlePower= calculateBattlePower(); } //================================================ // RANK SYSTEM //================================================ function updateArenaRank( player ){ let rating= player.rating; if(rating>=3000) player.rank="Grand Master"; else if(rating>=2400) player.rank="Diamond"; else if(rating>=1800) player.rank="Platinum"; else if(rating>=1400) player.rank="Gold"; else if(rating>=1100) player.rank="Silver"; else player.rank="Bronze"; } //================================================ // MATCH CLASS //================================================ class ArenaMatch{ constructor( playerA, playerB ){ this.playerA=playerA; this.playerB=playerB; this.time=180; this.active=true; this.scoreA=0; this.scoreB=0; } } //================================================ // MATCH MAKING //================================================ function findOpponent( player ){ let candidates=[]; for( let p of ArenaSystem.players.values() ){ if( p.id!=player.id ){ if( Math.abs( p.rating-player.rating )<300 ){ candidates.push(p); } } } if( candidates.length==0 ) return null; return candidates[ Math.floor( Math.random()* candidates.length ) ]; } function createArenaMatch( playerId ){ let player= ArenaSystem .players .get( playerId ); if(!player) return; let enemy= findOpponent( player ); if(!enemy) return; let match= new ArenaMatch( player, enemy ); ArenaSystem .matches .push( match ); return match; } //================================================ // PVP RESULT //================================================ function finishArenaMatch( match, winner ){ if(!match) return; let loser= winner==match.playerA? match.playerB: match.playerA; winner.wins++; loser.losses++; winner.rating+=25; loser.rating-=20; winner.kill++; loser.death++; updateArenaRank( winner ); updateArenaRank( loser ); match.active=false; } //================================================ // SEASON SYSTEM //================================================ function endArenaSeason(){ ArenaSystem .season++; for( let player of ArenaSystem.players.values() ){ player.rating=1000; player.rank="Bronze"; } } //================================================ // SEASON REWARD //================================================ ArenaSystem .seasonReward=[ "Exclusive Skin", "Legendary Gem", "Unique Title" ]; function claimSeasonReward( player ){ if( !player ) return; for( let reward of ArenaSystem.seasonReward ){ Inventory.add({ name:reward }); } } //================================================ // TOURNAMENT SYSTEM //================================================ const TournamentSystem={ active:false, players:[], round:1, winner:null }; function startTournament(){ TournamentSystem.active=true; TournamentSystem.round=1; TournamentSystem.players= Array.from( ArenaSystem.players.values() ); } function nextTournamentRound(){ TournamentSystem.round++; if( TournamentSystem.round>5 ){ TournamentSystem.active=false; TournamentSystem.winner= TournamentSystem.players[0]; } } //================================================ // ARENA BUFF //================================================ function applyArenaBonus( arenaPlayer ){ if(!arenaPlayer) return; let bonus= arenaPlayer.rating/100; Player.attack+=bonus; Player.maxHp+=bonus*10; } //================================================ // ARENA SAVE //================================================ function exportArenaData(){ return Array.from( ArenaSystem.players.values() ).map( p=>({ id:p.id, rating:p.rating, wins:p.wins, losses:p.losses, rank:p.rank }) ); } function importArenaData(data){ if(!data) return; for( let saved of data ){ let player= ArenaSystem .players .get( saved.id ); if(player){ player.rating= saved.rating; player.wins= saved.wins; player.losses= saved.losses; player.rank= saved.rank; } } } //================================================ // ADVANCED DUNGEON SYSTEM V13 // PROCEDURAL - RAID - ENDLESS //================================================ const DungeonSystem={ dungeons:new Map(), active:null, history:[], difficulty:"Normal" }; //================================================ // DUNGEON CLASS //================================================ class Dungeon{ constructor( id, name, difficulty ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.difficulty=difficulty; this.floor=1; this.maxFloor=10; this.rooms=[]; this.boss=null; this.reward=[]; this.completed=false; } } //================================================ // ROOM CLASS //================================================ class DungeonRoom{ constructor( type ){ this.type=type; this.cleared=false; this.monsters=[]; this.reward=[]; } } //================================================ // REGISTER DUNGEON //================================================ function registerDungeon( id, name, difficulty ){ let dungeon= new Dungeon( id, name, difficulty ); DungeonSystem .dungeons .set( id, dungeon ); return dungeon; } const WindCave= registerDungeon( "wind_cave", "Ancient Wind Cave", "Normal" ); WindCave.reward=[ "Wind Crystal", "Rare Equipment" ]; const DragonNest= registerDungeon( "dragon_nest", "Dragon Nest", "Hard" ); DragonNest.reward=[ "Dragon Scale", "Legendary Item" ]; //================================================ // PROCEDURAL GENERATOR //================================================ function generateDungeon( dungeon ){ dungeon.rooms=[]; let count= 5+ Math.floor( Math.random()*10 ); for( let i=0;i EndlessDungeon.highest ){ EndlessDungeon.highest= EndlessDungeon.floor; } spawnDungeonWave(); } function spawnDungeonWave(){ let amount= EndlessDungeon.floor*5; for( let i=0;i= PartySystem.maxMembers ) return; let member= Object.assign( new Companion(), JSON.parse( JSON.stringify(base) ) ); PartySystem .members .push( member ); updateFormation(); } //================================================ // REMOVE PARTY //================================================ function removePartyMember( id ){ PartySystem.members= PartySystem.members.filter( m=> m.id!=id ); } //================================================ // FORMATION //================================================ const FormationData={ Front:[ { x:-60, y:0 }, { x:-30, y:40 } ], Middle:[ { x:0, y:0 }, { x:20, y:50 } ], Back:[ { x:60, y:0 }, { x:80, y:40 } ] }; function updateFormation(){ let index=0; for( let member of PartySystem.members ){ let pos= FormationData [ PartySystem.formation ] [index%2]; member.position.x= Player.x+ pos.x; member.position.y= Player.y+ pos.y; index++; } } function changeFormation( type ){ if( FormationData[type] ){ PartySystem.formation= type; updateFormation(); } } //================================================ // COMPANION AI //================================================ function updateCompanionAI(){ for( let member of PartySystem.members ){ if(!member.alive) continue; let enemy= MonsterSystem.active.find( m=> Math.hypot( m.x-Player.x, m.y-Player.y )<250 ); if(!enemy) continue; switch( member.role ){ case"Tank": companionAttack( member, enemy ); break; case"DPS": companionAttack( member, enemy ); break; case"Healer": if( Player.hp< Player.maxHp*0.5 ){ healPlayer( member ); } break; } } } //================================================ // COMPANION ATTACK //================================================ function companionAttack( member, enemy ){ if(!enemy) return; enemy.hp-= member.attack; if( enemy.hp<=0 ){ killMonster( enemy ); } } //================================================ // HEAL AI //================================================ function healPlayer( member ){ let amount= 50+ member.level*10; Player.hp+=amount; if( Player.hp> Player.maxHp ) Player.hp= Player.maxHp; } //================================================ // PARTY SKILL //================================================ function registerPartySkill( name, effect ){ PartySystem .partySkills .push({ name:name, effect:effect }); } registerPartySkill( "Unity Attack", ()=>{ Player.attack*=1.2; } ); registerPartySkill( "Guardian Spirit", ()=>{ Player.defense*=1.3; } ); //================================================ // PARTY LEVEL //================================================ function levelParty(){ for( let member of PartySystem.members ){ member.level++; member.maxHp+=100; member.attack+=20; } } //================================================ // DRAW PARTY //================================================ function drawParty(){ for( let member of PartySystem.members ){ ctx.fillStyle= member.role=="Tank"? "blue": member.role=="Healer"? "yellow": "red"; ctx.fillRect( member.position.x-10, member.position.y-10, 20, 20 ); ctx.fillStyle="white"; ctx.font="12px Arial"; ctx.fillText( member.name, member.position.x-25, member.position.y-20 ); } } //================================================ // SAVE PARTY //================================================ function exportPartyData(){ return{ members: PartySystem.members.map( m=>m.id ), formation: PartySystem.formation }; } function importPartyData(data){ if(!data) return; PartySystem.members=[]; for( let id of data.members||[] ){ addPartyMember( id ); } PartySystem.formation= data.formation|| "Balanced"; } //================================================ // ADVANCED SKILL SYSTEM V15 // ACTIVE - PASSIVE - ULTIMATE - AWAKENING //================================================ const AdvancedSkillSystem={ skills:new Map(), playerSkills:new Map(), cooldowns:{}, awakening:false }; //================================================ // SKILL CLASS //================================================ class AdvancedSkill{ constructor( id, name, type ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.type=type; this.level=1; this.maxLevel=10; this.damage=0; this.manaCost=0; this.cooldown=0; this.effect=null; this.evolution=null; this.awakened=false; } } //================================================ // REGISTER SKILL //================================================ function registerSkill( id, name, type ){ let skill= new AdvancedSkill( id, name, type ); AdvancedSkillSystem .skills .set( id, skill ); return skill; } //================================================ // WARRIOR SKILLS //================================================ const powerSlash= registerSkill( "power_slash", "Power Slash", "Active" ); powerSlash.damage=150; powerSlash.cooldown=5; powerSlash.manaCost=20; powerSlash.effect= ()=>{ damageArea( 150 ); }; const ironBody= registerSkill( "iron_body", "Iron Body", "Passive" ); ironBody.effect= ()=>{ Player.defense+=50; }; const dragonRage= registerSkill( "dragon_rage", "Dragon Rage", "Ultimate" ); dragonRage.damage=1000; dragonRage.cooldown=60; dragonRage.effect= ()=>{ Player.attack*=2; }; //================================================ // MAGE SKILL //================================================ const fireMeteor= registerSkill( "fire_meteor", "Fire Meteor", "Active" ); fireMeteor.damage=500; fireMeteor.cooldown=10; fireMeteor.effect= ()=>{ damageArea( 500 ); }; //================================================ // LEARN SKILL //================================================ function learnSkill( id ){ let skill= AdvancedSkillSystem .skills .get( id ); if(!skill) return; AdvancedSkillSystem .playerSkills .set( id, skill ); } //================================================ // USE SKILL //================================================ function useSkill( id ){ let skill= AdvancedSkillSystem .playerSkills .get( id ); if(!skill) return; let now= Date.now(); let last= AdvancedSkillSystem .cooldowns[id] ||0; if( now-last < skill.cooldown*1000 ) return; AdvancedSkillSystem .cooldowns[id]=now; if( skill.effect ){ skill.effect(); } } //================================================ // LEVEL SKILL //================================================ function upgradeSkill( id ){ let skill= AdvancedSkillSystem .playerSkills .get( id ); if(!skill) return; if( skill.level>=skill.maxLevel ) return; skill.level++; skill.damage*=1.1; } //================================================ // COMBO SYSTEM //================================================ const ComboSystem={ sequence:[], timer:0 }; function addComboSkill( skillId ){ ComboSystem .sequence .push( skillId ); if( ComboSystem.sequence.length>=3 ){ activateCombo(); } } function activateCombo(){ let bonus= ComboSystem.sequence.length*100; damageArea( bonus ); ComboSystem.sequence=[]; } //================================================ // SKILL EVOLUTION //================================================ function evolveSkill( id ){ let skill= AdvancedSkillSystem .skills .get( id ); if(!skill) return; if( skill.level<10 ) return; skill.evolution= true; skill.damage*=2; } //================================================ // AWAKENING SYSTEM //================================================ const AwakeningSystem={ class:"", level:0, skills:[] }; function awakenClass( className ){ AwakeningSystem.class= className; AwakeningSystem.level=1; AdvancedSkillSystem .awakening=true; } function awakenSkill( id ){ let skill= AdvancedSkillSystem .skills .get( id ); if(!skill) return; skill.awakened=true; skill.damage*=3; skill.name+=" Awakened"; } //================================================ // DAMAGE AREA //================================================ function damageArea( damage ){ for( let monster of MonsterSystem.active ){ let distance= Math.hypot( monster.x-Player.x, monster.y-Player.y ); if( distance<120 ){ monster.hp-=damage; if( monster.hp<=0 ){ killMonster( monster ); } } } } //================================================ // SKILL HUD DATA //================================================ function getSkillData(){ return Array.from( AdvancedSkillSystem .playerSkills .values() ).map( s=>({ name:s.name, level:s.level, type:s.type }) ); } //================================================ // SAVE SKILL //================================================ function exportSkillData(){ return{ skills: Array.from( AdvancedSkillSystem.playerSkills.keys() ), awakening: AdvancedSkillSystem.awakening }; } function importSkillData(data){ if(!data) return; for( let id of data.skills||[] ){ learnSkill( id ); } AdvancedSkillSystem.awakening= data.awakening; } //================================================ // ADVANCED CLASS SYSTEM V16 // JOB CHANGE - AWAKENING - CLASS EQUIPMENT //================================================ const AdvancedClassSystem={ classes:new Map(), current:null, history:[], awakening:false }; //================================================ // CLASS DATA //================================================ class HeroClass{ constructor( id, name, tier ){ this.id=id; this.name=name; this.tier=tier; this.level=1; this.skills=[]; this.passives=[]; this.equipment=[]; this.stats={}; } } //================================================ // REGISTER CLASS //================================================ function registerClass( id, name, tier ){ let heroClass= new HeroClass( id, name, tier ); //================================================ // SECOND JOB //================================================ const knightClass= registerClass( "holy_knight", "Holy Knight", 2 ); knightClass.stats={ attack:220, defense:300, hp:4000 }; knightClass.skills=[ "shield_guard", "holy_strike" ]; const berserkerClass= registerClass( "berserker", "Berserker", 2 ); berserkerClass.stats={ attack:500, defense:100 }; berserkerClass.skills=[ "rage", "blood_slash" ]; //================================================ // SELECT CLASS //================================================ function selectClass( id ){ let heroClass= AdvancedClassSystem .classes .get( id ); if(!heroClass) return; AdvancedClassSystem.current= heroClass; AdvancedClassSystem .history .push( id ); applyClassStats( heroClass ); } //================================================ // APPLY CLASS STATS //================================================ function applyClassStats( heroClass ){ for( let stat in heroClass.stats ){ if( Player[stat]!=undefined ){ Player[stat]+= heroClass.stats[stat]; } } } //================================================ // JOB CHANGE //================================================ function jobChange( newClass ){ let target= AdvancedClassSystem .classes .get( newClass ); if(!target) return; if( target.tier<= AdvancedClassSystem.current.tier ) return; AdvancedClassSystem.current= target; applyClassStats( target ); unlockClassSkills( target ); } //================================================ // UNLOCK CLASS SKILL //================================================ function unlockClassSkills( heroClass ){ for( let skill of heroClass.skills ){ learnSkill( skill ); } } //================================================ // CLASS PASSIVE //================================================ function registerClassPassive( classId, passive ){ let heroClass= AdvancedClassSystem .classes .get( classId ); if(!heroClass) return; heroClass.passives.push( passive ); } registerClassPassive( "warrior", ()=>{ Player.maxHp+=1000; } ); registerClassPassive( "mage", ()=>{ Player.mana+=500; } ); //================================================ // CLASS EQUIPMENT //================================================ class ClassEquipment{ constructor( id, name, classId ){ this.id=id; } function applyAwakeningBonus(){ for( let stat in HeroAwakeningSystem.bonus ){ if( Player[stat]!=undefined ){ Player[stat]+= HeroAwakeningSystem .bonus[stat]; } } } //================================================ // SAVE CLASS //================================================ function exportClassData(){ return{ class: AdvancedClassSystem.current? AdvancedClassSystem.current.id:null, history: AdvancedClassSystem.history, awakening: HeroAwakeningSystem.stage }; } function importClassData(data){ if(!data) return; if( data.class ) selectClass( data.class ); AdvancedClassSystem.history= data.history||[]; HeroAwakeningSystem.stage= data.awakening||0; }
